A1 mini Introduction

---

This page introduces the key components of the A1 Mini 3D printer, which will help you understand the printer.

## [¶](#how-does-the-a1-mini-work) How does the A1 mini work?

The A1 mini 3D printer is a machine that uses plastic filament to create 3D objects. It can transform digital models into physical objects. The working principle of the A1 mini typically involves the following steps:

### [¶](#creation-or-acquisition-of-a-digital-model) Creation or acquisition of a digital model:

First, you need to create a 3D model or acquire an existing digital model. This can be done through computer-aided design software, online resources, or other sources.

### [¶](#slicing) Slicing

Next, Bambu Studio slices the digital model into multiple thin layers. The information for each layer is automatically converted into a language that the printer can understand, known as "G-code." This code instructs the printer on the movement paths and speeds of the various axes during the printing process.

### [¶](#preparing-the-printer) Preparing the printer

Before starting the printing process, you must prepare the printer. This includes loading the printing filament, placing the build plate, and calibrating the printer's parameters to ensure print quality.

### [¶](#printing) Printing

Once everything is prepared, the A1 mini gradually prints layer by layer onto the print platform, gradually building the object.

## [¶](#the-motion-system-of-the-a1-mini) The motion system of the A1 mini

![](/n1/manual/intro-a1-mini/en-picture/xyz-vv.png)

The A1 mini utilizes a Cartesian coordinate motion system, which consists of the following components:

### [¶](#x-axis-motion) X-axis motion

The X-axis motion comprises a horizontal guide rail and a crossbeam. The printhead is fixed on a sliding block that can freely slide along the horizontal guide rail. By controlling the left and right movement of the sliding block, the printhead can be accurately positioned in the X-axis direction.

### [¶](#y-axis-motion) Y-axis motion

The Y-axis motion comprises a horizontal guide rail and a print platform. The print platform is on the guide rail and can move back and forth along the Y-axis. By controlling the forward and backward movement of the print platform, control for printing in the Y-axis direction is achieved.

### [¶](#z-axis-motion) Z-axis motion

The Z-axis motion is used to control the vertical position of the printhead relative to the print platform. It comprises a lead screw, nut, coupling, and stepper motor. The stepper motor drives the lead screw to rotate through the coupling, thereby controlling the Z-axis motion of the tool head.

The A1 mini can precisely position the printhead in all three directions by controlling the X-axis, Y-axis, and Z-axis motion. Typically, these motions are controlled by motors and corresponding driver circuits. The print head's motion path and speed can be controlled by reading the print file through the machine controller and sending instructions.

## [¶](#tool-head-module) Tool head module

The tool head unit is one of the key components of the A1 mini. The tool head module mainly consists of the following components: hotend assembly, extruder unit, cutter unit, filament hub, and filament sensor.

![](/n1/manual/intro-a1-mini/en-picture/head-hotend.png)

### [¶](#hotend-assembly) Hotend assembly

The hotend assembly is a core component of the toolhead module, consisting of a hotend heating assembly, a hotend (integrated with a nozzle and a heat sink), a hotend fan, and a hotend silicone sock. The hotend assembly is responsible for heating the filament to a specific temperature, melting it, and depositing it in thin layers to generate the object.

Different filaments require different heating temperatures. For example, PLA material is printed at lower temperatures around 210-220°C, while PETG material requires higher temperatures around 250-260°C. Like the classic X1 series, the Bambu Lab A1 mini printer has an integrated hotend design that combines the nozzle and heat sink, enabling a faster heating effect than a regular hotend.

Additionally, it facilitates quick hotend replacement without unplugging tiny connectors or searching for a reliable screwdriver.

![](/n1/manual/intro-a1-mini/en-picture/hotend-assembly.png)

### [¶](#extruder-unit) Extruder unit

The extruder motor and extruder gears are essential components of the extruder unit. The extruder motor pulls the filament from the spool and feeds it into the hotend. Once heated and melted, the filament is extruded through the nozzle to create the printed model. The extruder motor is critical in accurately controlling the length of filament extruded through the hotend and is a core component of a 3D printer.

![](/n1/manual/intro-a1-mini/en-picture/extruder.png)

In the extruder unit, this small cover can be removed to observe the situation of filament clogging in the extruder gear.

![](/n1/manual/intro-a1-mini/en-picture/extruder-clog1.png)

The extruder gear can also be observed from the side by releasing the cutter lever. When filament powder accumulates in the extruder gear, this position can be used to clean the residue.

![](/n1/manual/intro-a1-mini/en-picture/extruder-clo2.png)

The filament hub can be carefully removed from the top (please be cautious when pulling the cable of the filament sensor), allowing observation from the top to check if the extruder gear is clogged.

![](/n1/manual/intro-a1-mini/en-picture/extruder-clo3.png)

### [¶](#cutter) Cutter

![](/n1/manual/intro-a1-mini/en-picture/cutter.png)

The cutter lever is installed on the tool head to secure the cutter blade and the magnet for position detection. The blade is driven to cut through the filament during the cutting process by pressing the handle. After cutting, the blade handle automatically rebounds.

The lever has a magnet, and a corresponding Hall sensor is inside the tool head at the same horizontal position as the magnet. Therefore, the printer can sense the position of the cutter lever through the Hall sensor.

### [¶](#filament-hub-and-filament-sensor) Filament hub and filament sensor

The PTFE tubes are connected to the filament hub on the tool head. This allows the toolhead to accommodate four different materials, enabling multi-color printing combined with a multi-color printing module. In addition, the filament hub contains a magnet inside, which works in conjunction with a filament entanglement hall sensor to check for filament entanglement. When there is excessive resistance during filament feeding, the filament hub is pulled down, causing the magnet to move closer to the Hall sensor and triggering a filament entanglement alert.

The filament sensor board includes circuits for filament feeding detection, filament cutting detection, and filament entanglement detection. The filament feeding detection is used to check if the filament is present in the extruder, the filament cutting detection controls the cutting action, and the filament entanglement detection is used to check for filament entanglement.

![](/n1/manual/intro-a1-mini/%E4%BA%94%E9%80%9A%E7%BB%84%E4%BB%B6-en.png)

## [¶](#cooling-system) Cooling System

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![](/n1/manual/intro-a1-mini/en-picture/part-cooling-fan1.png) | ![](/n1/manual/intro-a1-mini/en-picture/part-cooling-fan2.png) |

Efficient cooling of the printed model is essential for fast printing. The A1 mini has a custom 5015 centrifugal fan, featuring two opposing air outlets. This design directs the cooling air precisely towards the vicinity of the nozzle, ensuring rapid cooling of the printed parts.

## [¶](#heatbed-unit) Heatbed unit

![](/n1/manual/intro-a1-mini/en-picture/heatbed.png)

The main function of the heatbed is to heat the printing surface to assist in better adhesion of the printed layers to the build plate. Without heating the printing surface, the first layer of filament printed on the heatbed may not adhere well enough, leading to warping or even detachment of the printed object during subsequent printing processes. During the operation of the A1 mini printer, the heating temperature of the bed is controlled based on the type of printing filament used and can reach a maximum of 80°C.

Additionally, the heatbed serves as the Y-axis movement component of the printer. The Y-axis motor is connected to the heatbed via a timing belt, driving the heatbed to move along the Y-axis for printing in the Y-direction. The X-axis movement is accomplished by the X-axis motor driving the tool head through a timing belt. Both axes coordinate, allowing the printer nozzle to move in the XY direction on the heatbed surface.

## [¶](#heatbed-nozzle-wiper-and-purge-wiper) Heatbed nozzle wiper and Purge wiper

![](/n1/manual/intro-a1-mini/en-picture/wiper1.png)

## [¶](#purge-wiper) Purge wiper

![](/n1/manual/intro-a1-mini/en-picture/wiper2.png)

The purge wiper is specifically designed to efficiently handle the waste filament discharged from the nozzle. Its functions include collecting and conveying the waste filament and ensuring proper disposal to maintain continuous and smooth printing operations. This device plays a crucial role in various stages of the printing process, including printer setup, pause and resume printing, filament loading, and unloading, as well as filament switching and purging during multi-color printing.

## [¶](#heatbed-nozzle-wiper) Heatbed nozzle wiper

![](/n1/manual/intro-a1-mini/en-picture/wiper3.png)

The heatbed nozzle wiper is designed to clean the nozzle before starting a print job. After each print task is initiated, the cleaning is automatically performed without manual intervention. The cleaning action includes coarse wiping and fine wiping. Coarse wiping is primarily used to clean residual waste on the hot nozzle, while fine wiping ensures a smooth and clean nozzle surface. The A1 mini printer comes pre-installed with a heatbed nozzle wiper on the heatbed. It's important to note that replacing the nozzle wiper is a normal maintenance procedure. You can purchase replacement parts from the official store if the nozzle wiper gets damaged. (link will be added)

## [¶](#build-plate) Build plate

![](/n1/manual/intro-a1-mini/en-picture/buuid-plate.png)

The A1 mini supports two flexible build plates that can be magnetically attached to the heatbed.

## [¶](#bambu-textured-pei-plate) Bambu Textured PEI Plate

The Textured PEI Plate (in the package list) is made by coating PEI material on a stainless steel plate. It can print various filaments such as PLA, PETG, and TPU, and does not require adhesive. After printing, waiting for the heatbed to cool down before removing the printed model is recommended. Regular cleaning of the print surface is advised to maintain optimal adhesion. Additionally, it is recommended to minimize direct contact of fingers with the print surface to prevent skin oils from affecting adhesion.

## [¶](#bambu-smooth-pei-plate) Bambu Smooth PEI Plate

The Smooth PEI Plate (sold separately, available in the store) is created by attaching a PEI film to a spring steel plate using a 3M high-temperature adhesive. This print surface is ideal for printing PLA without any adhesive, but applying solid or liquid glue is recommended for printing another filament to reduce the risk of damaging the PEI film.

## [¶](#belt-tensioner) Belt Tensioner

The A1 mini has belt tensioners for both the X and Y axes.

### [¶](#x-axis-belt-tensioner) X-axis Belt Tensioner

![](/n1/manual/intro-a1-mini/en-picture/x-tensioner-v1.png)

The X-axis belt tensioner is located on the tool head, and an accelerometer sensor on the tool head monitors the tension force of the X-axis belt. If there is an abnormality in the tension force of the X-axis belt, the system will prompt you to adjust the tension. Please refer to the A1 mini's regular maintenance guide for instructions on tensioning the X-axis belt. (will be added)

### [¶](#y-axis-belt-tensioner) Y-axis Belt Tensioner

![](/n1/manual/intro-a1-mini/en-picture/y-tensioner-v.png)

The Y-axis belt tensioner is located at the rear of the machine and is equipped with an accelerometer to monitor the tension force of the Y-axis belt. This allows for continuous monitoring and adjustment of the Y-axis belt tension.

When there is an abnormality in the tension force of the Y-axis belt, the system will prompt that there is an issue with the Y-axis belt tension. Please refer to the A1 mini's regular maintenance guide for instructions on tensioning the Y-axis belt. (will be added)

## [¶](#electrical-components) Electrical Components

The A1 mini incorporates various electronic components, and here is a brief overview of some of the most important components and their functions:

### [¶](#circuit-board) Circuit board

The mainboard contains a low-power SoC (System-on-Chip) with a dual-core processor, Wi-Fi baseband, Bluetooth baseband, RF module, and other peripherals. It also includes a dual-core MCU and a single-core MCU. Additionally, it features stepper motor drivers and connectors necessary for connecting different electronic devices to the printer. The SoC handles the user interaction and the communication between the slicing software/mobile application and the printer. The MCU controls the actual heating and movement of the printer after receiving G-code instructions from the CPU.

### [¶](#camera) Camera

The printer integrates a camera used for spaghetti detection (to be supported in future firmware updates) and real-time monitoring of prints using the Bambu Slicer or Bambu Handy application. In addition to the monitoring function, the camera can record videos of the prints to the micro SD card and generate time-lapse videos. The machine is not equipped with night vision, but the camera module has an auxiliary light to illuminate the print.

## [¶](#control-module-functions) Control Module Functions

### [¶](#a1-mini-auto-bed-leveling) A1 mini Auto Bed Leveling

The A1 mini's auto bed leveling involves the following steps:

### [¶](#touch-probe-detection) Touch Probe Detection

A1 mini has a feature to measure the force exerted on the hotend, which can be used to detect contact between the toolhead and the heatbed. Combining this with the up-and-down movement of the Z-axis, the A1 mini can directly measure the height of a specific point on the heated bed relative to the center of the bed.

### [¶](#height-mapping) Height Mapping

By touching the heatbed with the extruder head, the A1 mini performs height measurements at 36 points (6x6 grid) on the bed, creating a height map of the bed. This height map records the height of each point on the 6x6 grid in relation to the bed.

### [¶](#compensation-adjustment) Compensation Adjustment

During printing, the printer automatically adjusts the height of the Z-axis at different XY coordinates based on the height map. This ensures a consistent distance between the extruder head and the bed, allowing the printing material to adhere uniformly to the print bed.

Through the auto bed leveling function, the A1 mini can automatically detect and adjust the height of the print bed before printing and during the printing process. This improves print quality and accuracy by addressing issues caused by an uneven print bed surface, enhancing print success rates and consistency.

## [¶](#a1-mini-resonance-compensation) A1 mini Resonance Compensation

Resonance compensation aims to mitigate or eliminate instability and quality issues caused by mechanical vibrations or resonance during printing.

During the operation of a 3D printer, every acceleration and deceleration excites the resonant modes of the printer itself, resulting in subtle oscillations. These vibrations can have a negative impact on print quality, such as ripples, vibration patterns, or distortion on the surface of printed parts.

To eliminate these resonance effects, the A1 mini implements resonance compensation technology, involving the following steps:

### [¶](#vibration-analysis) Vibration Analysis

Using an accelerometer (for the X-axis) or an eddy current sensor (for the Y-axis), the printer can induce vibrations in the motors at different frequencies and capture the actual vibration curves of the XY axes. These curves are then analyzed to identify the resonance modes of the XY axes.

### [¶](#compensation-parameter-calculation) Compensation Parameter Calculation

Based on the resonance mode vibration analysis results, the printer's system uses specific compensation algorithms to calculate compensation parameters.

### [¶](#dynamic-adjustment) Dynamic Adjustment

During printing, the resonance compensation function can dynamically apply compensation to the motion controller based on real-time motion control commands. This counteracts resonance modes and ensures that the movement of the extruder's nozzle aligns with the expected G-code.

The A1 mini reduces the impact of mechanical vibrations and resonance during the printing process through resonance compensation technology, thereby improving print quality and accuracy. It minimizes ripples, vibration patterns, or distortion on the surface of printed parts, resulting in smoother and more precise print results. This is particularly important for applications that require high precision and stability.

## [¶](#flow-dynamics-calibration) Flow Dynamics Calibration

![](/n1/manual/intro-a1-mini/en-picture/flow-dynamic.jpg)

During the 3D printing process, accurately controlling material flow is crucial for print quality. Incorrect flow settings can result in issues such as inaccurate part dimensions, poor interlayer adhesion, and surface quality problems. The purpose of dynamic flow calibration is to measure the dynamic response characteristics of the material at different flow rates, calculate the material's hysteresis during the extrusion process, and perform real-time hysteresis compensation during printing. This allows the printer to achieve uniform extrusion even at high speeds and accelerations, providing more accurate and consistent print results.

Implementing dynamic flow calibration typically involves the following steps:

### [¶](#dynamic-hysteresis-measurement) Dynamic Hysteresis Measurement

The A1 mini utilizes sensors to measure the flow rate variation of the material during the calibration process.

### [¶](#dynamic-flow-calibration) Dynamic Flow Calibration

The printer automatically calculates compensation parameters using the flow rate variation data, similar to "pressure advance" in Klipper or "linear advance" in Marlin.

### [¶](#real-time-adjustment) Real-time Adjustment

During the printing process, the printer compensates for the extrusion volume based on real-time flow rate requests, ensuring that the material flow from the extruder remains synchronized with the high-speed motion of the XY axes. This ensures consistent flow rates across different printing areas and layers.

Through dynamic flow calibration, the A1 mini can dynamically adjust the material flow during printing to adapt to different printing conditions and requirements. This helps improve print quality, accuracy, and consistency, ensuring that printed parts have correct dimensions and quality while reducing issues caused by inaccurate material flow.

## [¶](#active-motor-noise-canceling) Active Motor Noise Canceling

![](/n1/manual/intro-a1-mini/en-picture/noise.jpg)

Motor noise is typically caused by mechanical vibrations, motor driving, fan operation, or extruder movement in a 3D printer. These noises can not only be disruptive to the operator but also affect the comfort of the surrounding environment. The motor noise suppression function aims to reduce the overall printing noise by suppressing the noise generated by the motors during the printing process.

Motor noise reduction is achieved by measuring the undesirable characteristics within the motor and calculating the compensation amount in real time based on the motor's current operating state during printing. This compensation is then applied to the motor, generating reverse vibrations within the motor to counteract the vibrations caused by the motor's imperfections. Motor noise calibration is available in the calibration menu, allowing users to recalibrate the motor noise as needed.

## [¶](#end-notes) End Notes

> We hope that the detailed guide we shared with you was helpful and informative.
>
> We want to ensure that you can perform it safely and effectively. If you have any concerns or questions regarding the process described in this article, we encourage you to reach out to our friendly customer service team before starting the operation. Our team is always ready to help you and answer any questions you may have.
>
> [Click here to open a new ticket in our Support Page](https://bambulab.com/en/my/support/tickets?from=5).
>
> We will do our best to respond promptly and provide you with the assistance you need.

First print with an external spool

---

## [¶](#video-guide) Video guide

## [¶](#printing-preparation) Printing Preparation

### [¶](#using-the-included-filaments) Using the included filaments

![](/n1/manual/first-print-with-external-spool/%E8%B5%A0%E9%80%81%E8%80%97%E6%9D%90.jpg)

#### [¶](#h-1-insert-the-filament) 1. Insert the filament

Insert the filament into the PTFE tube and push it into the toolhead until it can no longer move forward.

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![](/n1/manual/first-print-with-external-spool/%E6%89%8B%E5%8A%A8%E6%8E%A8%E5%85%A5.jpg) | ![](/n1/manual/first-print-with-external-spool/%E9%80%81%E5%85%A5%E8%80%97%E6%9D%90.jpg) |

#### [¶](#h-2-load-the-filament) 2. Load the filament

1. Click **Filament** on the printer screen. At this point, the green indicator on the toolhead will be illuminated on the printer's screen.

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![](/n1/manual/first-print-with-external-spool/%E7%82%B9%E5%87%BBfilament.jpg) | ![](/n1/manual/first-print-with-external-spool/green_dot.jpg) |

2. Click **Load** on the screen.

![](/n1/manual/first-print-with-external-spool/click_load.jpg)

3. Once the nozzle starts heating up to 250 ℃, manually push the filament into the toolhead again.

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![](/n1/manual/first-print-with-external-spool/loading.jpg) | ![](/n1/manual/first-print-with-external-spool/%E6%89%8B%E5%8A%A8%E6%8E%A8%E5%85%A5.jpg) |

4. Observe the nozzle to ensure that the extruder is properly extruding the filament. After visually confirming that the nozzle smoothly extruded the filament, select **Done** on the screen to complete the loading process.

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![](/n1/manual/first-print-with-external-spool/%E8%80%97%E6%9D%90%E6%8C%A4%E5%87%BA.jpg) | ![](/n1/manual/first-print-with-external-spool/retry_or_done.jpg) |

Select **Retry** and repeat pushing the filament again in case you are unsure whether the filament was successfully extruded.

### [¶](#using-the-spool) Using the spool

#### [¶](#h-1-install-the-spool) 1. Install the spool

Place the filament spool on the bracket, paying attention to the direction.

![](/n1/manual/first-print-with-external-spool/prepare1-external.png)

#### [¶](#h-2-load-the-filament-1) 2. Load the filament

After installing the filament spool, insert one end of the filament into the PTFE tube and push it into the toolhead until it can no longer move forward. At this point, the green indicator on the toolhead will be illuminated on the printer's screen.

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![](/n1/manual/first-print-with-external-spool/prepare2-external.png) | ![](/n1/manual/first-print-with-external-spool/prepare3-external.png) |

Click "Load" on the screen.

![](/n1/manual/first-print-with-external-spool/prepare4-external.png)

Once the nozzle starts heating up to 250 ℃, manually push the filament into the toolhead again.

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![](/n1/manual/first-print-with-external-spool/prepare5-external.png) | ![](/n1/manual/first-print-with-external-spool/prepare6-external.png) |

Observe the nozzle to ensure that the extruder is properly extruding the filament.

![](/n1/manual/first-print-with-external-spool/prepare7-external.png)

After visually confirming that the nozzle smoothly extruded the filament, select "Done" on the screen to complete the loading process.

![](/n1/manual/first-print-with-external-spool/prepare8-external.png)

Select "Retry" and repeat pushing the filament again in case you are unsure whether the filament was successfully extruded.

## [¶](#start-printing) Start printing

**After ensuring the filament is loaded and the build plate is in place, you can proceed with printing.**

### [¶](#initiate-a-print-job-using-bambu-studio) Initiate a print job using Bambu Studio

Click here to [download Bambu Studio](https://bambulab.com/en/download/studio).

![](/n1/manual/first-print-with-ams/start-print2.png)

Slice and send the print.

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![](/n1/manual/first-print-with-external-spool/start-print1-external.png) | ![](/n1/manual/first-print-with-external-spool/start-print2-external.png) |

### [¶](#initiate-the-print-using-the-built-in-file) Initiate the print using the built-in file

Click "Print files" on the home page to access the built-in files on the microSD card.

![](/n1/manual/first-print-with-external-spool/start-print3-external.png)

Select the model you would like to print.

![](/n1/manual/first-print-with-external-spool/start-print4-external.png)

It is recommended to enable the bed leveling and flow dynamics calibration function.

![](/n1/manual/first-print-with-external-spool/start-print5-external.png)

Click "Print."

![](/n1/manual/first-print-with-external-spool/start-print6-external.png)

## [¶](#how-to-refill) How to refill?

If the filament is used up during printing, you can remove the empty filament spool from the external bracket and install a new one. Refer to the above **"Printing Preparation"** operation to refill the filament.

## [¶](#how-to-replace-the-filament) How to replace the filament?

If you would like to replace the filament, you need to pause the current printing task, first remove the filament in the toolhead, and then load the new filament.

### [¶](#h-1-unload-the-filament) 1. Unload the filament

Click "Unload" on the screen, and the printer automatically heat the hotend and cut off the filament.

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![](/n1/manual/first-print-with-external-spool/appen1-external.png) | ![](/n1/manual/first-print-with-external-spool/appen2-external.png) |

### [¶](#h-2-manually-remove-the-filament-from-the-extruder) 2. Manually remove the filament from the extruder

After completing the previous step, it is necessary to remove the filament to complete the unloading process manually.

Please follow the APP/Studio prompts and promptly manually remove the filament. If the nozzle temperature remains above 142°C without dropping, it indicates that there is remaining filament in the extruder, which means the unloading process is not completed.

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![](/n1/manual/first-print-with-external-spool/appen3-external.png) | ![](/n1/manual/first-print-with-external-spool/appen4-external.png) |

If you didn't remove the filament in a timely manner, please click "Retry" on the screen and try to remove them again.

### [¶](#h-3-load-the-filament) 3. Load the filament

Please follow the **"Printing Preparation"** operation mentioned above after completing the "Unload" procedure.

## [¶](#end-notes) **End Notes**

> We hope the detailed guide provided has been helpful and informative.
>
> If this guide does not solve your problem, please submit a [technical ticket](https://bambulab.com/en/my/support/tickets/create?from=5), we will answer your questions and provide assistance.
>
> If you have any suggestions or feedback on this Wiki, please leave a message in the comment area. Thank you for your support and attention!

A1 mini FAQ

---

## [¶](#introduction) Introduction

We have categorized the FAQ of A1 mini into three sections: **product specifications, product features, and product usage-related**. You can easily navigate to the desired module using the sidebar on the left.

[AMS lite FAQ](/en/ams-lite/manual/faq)

## [¶](#product-usage-related) Product usage related

### [¶](#what-is-the-recommended-ambient-temperature-and-humidity-for-the-a1-mini-printer) What is the recommended ambient temperature and humidity for the A1 mini printer?

The recommended ambient temperature of the A1mini is 10℃ - 30℃ (50°F - 86°F). The recommended humidity level is under 85%.

### [¶](#is-cf-filament-okay-to-print-using-a1-mini) Is CF filament OKAY to print using A1 mini?

The A1 mini printer comes with a pre-installed 0.4mm stainless steel nozzle. It is not recommended to use stainless steel nozzles for printing materials that contain carbon fiber or glass fiber, such as PLA-CF/GF, PETG-CF/GF, etc., to minimize excessive nozzle wear. However, if you replace the nozzle with a hardened steel material, you can print CF-based filaments on the A1 mini. If you intend to use the A1 mini with the AMS lite, please ensure that you follow the filament requirements specified by the AMS lite as well.

### [¶](#can-i-use-the-ams-on-the-a1-mini) Can I use the AMS on the A1 mini?

Yes. The A1 mini was originally compatible with AMS lite. To use AMS, AMS 2 Pro, or AMS HT with the A1 mini, you need the [AMS hub](/en/a1/manual/ams-connection-guide) accessory to regulate filament tension, as these AMS units provide assisted feeding during printing.

### [¶](#what-are-the-best-locations-to-position-the-a1-mini-and-ams-lite) What are the best locations to position the A1 mini and AMS lite?

The diagram shows that the recommended distance between the A1 mini and AMS lite is 50mm.

![](/n1/manual/faq/ams-lite%E6%91%86%E6%94%BE%E4%BD%8D%E7%BD%AE.png)

### [¶](#can-you-use-multiple-ams-lite-units-with-the-a1-mini-for-up-to-16-color-prints) Can you use multiple AMS lite units with the A1 mini for up to 16 color prints?

The A1 mini can only support one AMS lite, allowing a maximum of 4 colors for printing.

### [¶](#the-a1-mini-supports-a-maximum-of-one-ams-lite-so-why-is-an-extra-4-pin-connector-available) The A1 mini supports a maximum of one AMS lite, so why is an extra 4-pin connector available?

This interface is reserved for future product expansions and/or additional functionalities.

### [¶](#how-should-the-ptfe-tubes-be-oriented-for-the-least-resistance) How should the PTFE tubes be oriented for the least resistance?

Insert shorter 580mm PTFE tubes into slots 1 and 2, and longer 700mm PTFE tubes into slots 3 and 4. Simultaneously, insert the other ends of the PTFE tubes in slots 1 and 2 into the two slots on the left side of the filament hub, while slots 3 and 4 should be inserted into the two slots on the right side of the filament hub (when viewing the printer from the front to the back).

![](/n1/manual/faq/ptfe%E6%8F%92%E6%96%99%E9%A1%BA%E5%BA%8F.png)

### [¶](#what-is-the-overall-space-requirement-x-y-and-z-for-the-a1-mini-ams-lite) What is the overall space requirement X, Y, and Z for the A1 mini + AMS lite?

![](/n1/manual/faq/a1-mini-en.png)

### [¶](#how-much-space-should-be-reserved-when-moving-the-a1-mini-heatbed-to-the-front-and-back-of-the-printer) How much space should be reserved when moving the A1 mini heatbed to the front and back of the printer?

As shown in the diagram, when placing the printer, please leave sufficient, safe space for the heatbed to move in the Y direction, approximately 390 mm.

![](https://wiki.bambulab.com/n1/manual/unboxing-a1-mini-urey/space1.png)

### [¶](#what-different-plates-are-available-for-the-a1-mini) What different Plates are available for the A1 mini?

The A1 mini supports both a textured PEI plate and a smooth PEI plate. A textured PEI plate is included as an accessory when you unbox the printer. If you prefer a smooth PEI plate, you can purchase it from our [official online store](https://us.store.bambulab.com/products/bambu-smooth-pei-plate?id=42479379579016).

### [¶](#what-materials-can-be-printed-on-a-textured-pei-plate) What materials can be printed on a textured PEI plate?

The textured PEI plate is capable of printing a wide range of materials such as PLA, PETG, and TPU without the need for glue. After printing, it is recommended to allow the build plate to cool down completely before removing the printed model. To maintain optimal adhesion, it is advised to clean the build plate using warm water and regular soap.

### [¶](#what-materials-can-be-printed-on-a-smooth-pei-plate) What materials can be printed on a smooth PEI plate?

The smooth PEI plate features a flat surface and is capable of printing PLA, PETG, and TPU. This printing surface is especially suitable for printing PLA without the need for glue. However, when printing PETG and TPU, it is necessary to apply glue in either liquid or solid form.

### [¶](#can-we-replace-the-smooth-pei-build-plate-sticker-like-the-x1-and-p1-series) Can we replace the smooth PEI build plate sticker like the X1 and P1 series?

No, the smooth PEI build plate sticker is not replaceable.

### [¶](#can-the-a1-mini-print-without-a-microsd-card-use-only-bambu-studiohandy) Can the A1 mini print without a microSD card & use only Bambu Studio/Handy?

No, the A1 mini does not have built-in memory, so you need to insert a micro SD card to initiate a print. The printer comes with a pre-installed 32 GB micro SD card.

### [¶](#what-is-the-maximum-size-and-format-microsd-card-the-a1-mini-can-use) What is the maximum size and format microSD card the A1 mini can use?

The A1 mini supports a micro SD card of up to 2TB if formatted using the printer option. Please refer to [this page](/en/general/micro-sd-faq) for more information about micro SD card-related FAQs.

### [¶](#what-does-the-white-led-on-the-printer-body-above-the-screen-indicate) What does the white LED on the printer body above the screen indicate?

A flashing white LED indicates the presence of AI assistant messages that require attention, while a solid white light indicates that the system status is normal.

### [¶](#how-to-modify-the-printing-speed-of-the-a1-mini) How to modify the printing speed of the A1 mini?

You can set the print speed in Bambu Studio when slicing on the speed page. While printing, you can change the speed mode (Silent, Standard, Sport, Ludicrous) on the printer, Bambu Handy or Bambu Studio. It's important to note that Sport and Turbo require higher hotend temperatures for best results, and we recommend setting a higher temperature in the slicer.

### [¶](#how-does-the-printer-react-to-a-filament-runout) How does the printer react to a filament runout?

The A1 mini employs a filament hall sensor to detect if the filament has run out. It supports filament runout detection and can resume printing after filament replacement. When used with AMS lite, the A1 mini can be configured to utilize a backup filament as a substitute once the current filament runs out.

### [¶](#how-does-the-printer-react-to-power-loss) How does the printer react to Power Loss?

After powering on the printer again, you have the option to resume printing after a power loss. This feature allows the printer to continue and complete the previously interrupted print job.

### [¶](#can-you-turn-off-all-the-a1-mini-speaker-sounds) Can you turn off all the A1 mini speaker sounds?

Yes, you can turn off the sound in the printer settings.

### [¶](#is-pause-at-a-certain-layer-height-available) Is pause at a certain layer height available?

Yes, pause at a certain layer height is available and can be done in Bambu Studio.

### [¶](#is-a-spool-holder-included-when-a-customer-didnt-buy-the-combo-with-ams) Is a spool holder included when a customer didn't buy the combo with AMS?

The accessories for the A1 mini include a spool holder, which needs to be installed by the user upon unboxing. Please refer to [A1 mini Unboxing Guide](/en/a1-mini/manual/unboxing-a1-mini).

### [¶](#what-is-the-flow-rate-when-printing-tpu) What is the flow rate when printing TPU?

We have a [printing guide for TPU](/en/knowledge-sharing/tpu-printing-guide), and you can find more information in this document. Additionally, we recommend you refer to our filament guide to obtain more information on [filament settings parameters.](/en/general/filament-guide-material-table)

### [¶](#where-is-the-best-place-to-position-the-purge-tower) Where is the best place to position the purge tower?

Near the purge wiper - Left side (Thus avoiding waste or scaps getting onto the bed or z-axis and saving the toolhead's travel time during filament changes.)

### [¶](#is-it-ok-to-enclose-the-a1-mini) Is it OK to enclose the A1 mini?

We don't recommend enclosing the A1 mini.

### [¶](#what-is-the-firmware-upgrade-process) What is the firmware upgrade process?

Firmware upgrades will be published occasionally to add more features and patches to the software running on the machine. The printer will check for any available updates in the cloud and display a pop-up notification if there are.

### [¶](#does-the-a1-mini-have-auto-recovery-from-a-step-loss) Does the A1 mini have Auto-recovery from a step loss?

Yes, the A1 mini supports auto-recovery from a step loss; you can enable this feature on the print options page.

### [¶](#is-there-a-proper-tool-to-remove-the-tubes-from-the-tool-head) Is there a proper tool to remove the tubes from the tool head?

If you find removing the PTFE tube from the tool head inconvenient, you can print this model to assist with the operation.

[ptfe\_tool.3mf](/n1/p-maintenance/ptfe_tool.3mf)

### [¶](#how-to-turn-off-the-sound-effects-before-and-after-printing) How to turn off the sound effects before and after printing?

The sound effect can be turned off in 'Main menu - Settings - Print options.'

| ![](/n1/manual/ui-explanation/en-version/setting_2.jpg) | ![](/n1/manual/ui-explanation/en-version/setting_3.jpg) |
| --- | --- |

### [¶](#how-to-install-the-blade-for-the-heatbed-scraper) How to install the blade for the heatbed scraper?

Please refer to our installation video for instructions.

### [¶](#can-you-use-the-printer-without-any-internet-connection-at-all) Can you use the printer without any internet connection at all?

Yes, you can start the print from micro SD, please refer to the Wiki [Print from Micro SD Card](/en/x1/manual/print-from-sd-card).
Please note that it is not possible to update the firmware on the device without a wi-fi connection.

## [¶](#product-specifications-related) Product specifications related

### [¶](#what-materials-can-the-a1-mini-print) What materials can the A1 mini Print?

The A1 mini is capable of printing a wide range of materials available on the market that do not require an enclosure. These materials include PLA, PETG, TPU, and their corresponding support filaments. However, it is not recommended to print filaments such as ABS, ASA, PC, PA, and PET that require higher bed and chamber temperatures, otherwise warping, detaching from the heatbed, weakness in the Z direction and other issues can happen to the models.

### [¶](#what-is-the-max-hotendheatbed-temperature) What is the max hotend/heatbed temperature?

The maximum hotend temperature for the A1 mini is 300 ℃, while the maximum heatbed temperature is 80 ℃.

### [¶](#what-is-the-maximum-print-size-of-the-a1-mini) What is the maximum print size of the A1 mini?

180 \* 180 \*180 mm. For more [product specifications](https://bambulab.com/zh/a1-mini/tech-specs), please refer to this page.

### [¶](#is-the-nozzle-stainless-steel-or-hardened-steel) Is the nozzle stainless steel or hardened steel?

The printer is equipped with a pre-installed 0.4mm stainless steel nozzle. Additional nozzles will be available for purchase at our official store, including a 0.2mm stainless steel nozzle and hardened steel nozzles in 0.4mm, 0.6mm, and 0.8mm diameters. If you need these nozzles, you can conveniently purchase them from [our official store](https://us.store.bambulab.com/collections/accessories-hotends-a1-series).

### [¶](#how-quiet-is-the-a1-mini-when-printing) How quiet is the A1 mini when printing?

IIn silent mode, the operating noise level of the A1 mini is approximately 48 dB.

### [¶](#what-is-the-power-draw-of-the-a1-mini-in-printing-and-idle-mode) What is the power draw of the A1 mini in printing and idle mode?

The A1 mini consumes approximately 6 W of power in idle mode and an average of 57 W when printing. However, please note that power consumption can be significantly higher when the printer is heating the bed and hotend from ambient temperature during the printing process.

## [¶](#product-features-related) Product features related

### [¶](#how-can-i-level-the-bed) How can I level the bed?

Our printer supports the auto bed leveling feature. We suggest you check the heatbed leveling option before starting the print job. This way, the algorithm will quickly check five points to determine whether to level again or reuse the previous leveling data. There shouldn't be a need to level the heatbed manually.

### [¶](#is-bed-leveling-necessary-for-every-printing) Is bed leveling necessary for every printing?

We highly recommend enabling the auto bed leveling option for every print job, as magnetic build plates may not be placed in the exact same position with perfect precision after each print. Additionally, before the auto bed leveling, the printer will swiftly test five points to determine if it needs to level the bed again or if it can reuse the previous leveling data.

### [¶](#how-does-flow-calibration-work) How does flow calibration work?

During the purging process at the nozzle wiper, the hotend will detect the variations in extrusion force and use this information to calibrate the K value.

### [¶](#what-happens-in-a-partial-nozzle-clog-situation) What happens in a partial nozzle clog situation?

A partial nozzle clog can result in an incorrect K value. However, the good news is that future firmware updates will include support for manual flow dynamics calibration. Please refer to the [A1 Serise firmware release note](/en/a1/manual/a1-firmware-release-history).

### [¶](#is-dynamic-flow-calibration-needed-before-every-print) Is dynamic flow calibration needed before every print?

To ensure optimal printing results, it is recommended to calibrate the following aspects (although it is highly recommended to keep them consistent for every print):

1. If you introduce a new filament of different brands or models ;
2. If the nozzle is worn out, the friction in the nozzle will change;
3. If you replace the nozzle, as there is tolerance in the manufacturing;
4. If the filament is damp, moisture will change the viscosity of the filament.
5. If the max volumetric speed or print temperature is changed in the filament setting.

### [¶](#does-the-ams-lite-filament-hub-contain-a-hall-effect-sensor-for-each-filament-input) Does the AMS lite filament hub contain a hall effect sensor for each filament input?

No, the AMS lite filament hub is a purely mechanical structure without any electronic components. . It is not equipped with the capability to identify the specific slot from which the current filament is being used. In order for the printer to determine the slot from which the current material is sourced, it independently verifies the filament's position each time it loads or unloads the filament. For further elaboration, please consult the [AMS lite introduction](/en/ams-lite/manual/intro-ams-lite).

![](/n1/manual/faq/%E4%BA%94%E9%80%9A%E7%BB%84%E4%BB%B6.png)

## [¶](#end-notes) End Notes

> We hope that the detailed guide we shared with you was helpful and informative.
>
> We want to ensure that you can perform it safely and effectively. If you have any concerns or questions regarding the process described in this article, we encourage you to reach out to our friendly customer service team before starting the operation. Our team is always ready to help you and answer any questions you may have.
>
> [Click here to open a new ticket in our Support Page](https://bambulab.com/en/my/support/tickets?from=5).
>
> We will do our best to respond promptly and provide you with the assistance you need.

A1 mini Glossary

Detailed information related to the A1 mini hardware components, with links to purchase replacements

---

This page contains short explanations about each main components of the A1 mini and links to the Bambu Store from where you can purchase them.

If there is no link to the store, and the component is not available to purchase directly, please contact our [Customer Support](https://bambulab.com/en-us/support) team so we can assist.

Detailed guides for how to replace each part are available on the [main A1 mini Wiki page](/en/a1-mini) and in the product description of the store.

## [¶](#front-view) Front View

![](/glossary/a1_mini_glosssary_1.png)

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| 1. [Filament Hub](https://us.store.bambulab.com/products/ams-lite-filament-hub) | This component is used for connecting all the PTFE tubes to the Toolhead. It also contains the [filament sensor](https://us.store.bambulab.com/products/filament-sensor-a1-series). |
| 2. Toolhead | This component contains multiple parts like the [Extruder](https://us.store.bambulab.com/products/extruder-unit-a1-series), Hotend, [Part Cooling Fan](https://us.store.bambulab.com/products/part-cooling-fan-a1-series), [Toolhead Board](https://us.store.bambulab.com/collections/spare-parts-for-a1-series/products/toolhead-board-a1-series), Extruder Motor. |
| 3. [X-axis Linear Rail](https://us.store.bambulab.com/collections/spare-parts-for-a1-series/products/x-axis-assembly-a1-mini) | This is the linear rail which provides smooth motion for the Toolhead on the X-axis during printing. |
| 4. [Purge Wiper](https://us.store.bambulab.com/products/purge-wiper-a1-series) | This component helps with removing the purged filament before and during printing, to remove the purged material. |
| 5. [Hotend Silicone Sock](https://us.store.bambulab.com/products/hotend-silicone-sock-a1-series) | This component is attached to the [Hotend](https://us.store.bambulab.com/products/hotend-with-0-4-mm-nozzle-a1-series) to keep it clean and hold the temperature stable. |
| 6. [Build Plate](https://us.store.bambulab.com/products/bambu-textured-pei-plate?id=42479517925512) | This component is the surface which magnetically attaches to the Heatbed and the model is printed on. |
| 7. [Heatbed](https://us.store.bambulab.com/products/a1-mini-heatbed-unit) | This component moves on the Y-axis during printing holding the Build Plate magnetically attached. It also includes the [Nozzle Wipers](https://us.store.bambulab.com/collections/spare-parts-for-a1-series/products/heatbed-nozzle-wiper-a1-mini). |
| 8. Y-axis Linear Rail | This is the linear rail which provides smooth motion for the Heatbed on the Y-axis during printing. |
| 9. [USB C Cable](https://us.store.bambulab.com/collections/spare-parts-for-a1-series/products/bambu-usb-c-cable-a1-mini) |
| This component connects the Toolhead to the Mainboard. |
| 10. [Filament Cutter Lever](https://us.store.bambulab.com/products/filament-cutter-lever-a1-series) | This component provides the mechanical cutting force to the Filament Cutting Blade. |
| 11. [Camera](https://us.store.bambulab.com/collections/spare-parts-for-a1-series/products/live-view-camera-a1-mini) | This component allows remote 3D printing monitoring and viewing in Bambu Studio and Bambu Handy. |
| 12. Z-Axis Linear Rail | This is the linear rail which provides smooth motion for the X-axis on the Z-axis during printing. |
| 13. [Part Cooling Fan](https://us.store.bambulab.com/collections/spare-parts-for-a1-series/products/part-cooling-fan-a1-series) | This component cools the printed layer lines to solidify the filament quickly. |
| 14. [Nozzle](https://us.store.bambulab.com/products/bambu-hotend-a1-series) | This component is melting the filament before the printer lays down the layer lines of the 3D printed model. |
| 15. [Touchscreen](https://us.store.bambulab.com/collections/spare-parts-for-a1-series/products/touch-screen-a1-mini) | This component allows for touch interaction with the printer, and also displays information and error messages. |
| 16. AMS Ports | The AMS ports allow for the AMS lite to be connected to the printer and allow multi-color and multi-material printing. |
| 17. Micro SD Card | This is a memory device that holds the 3D printing files, Timelapse Videos and Log information. |

## [¶](#side-view) Side View

![](/glossary/a1_mini_glossary_2.jpg)

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| 1. [X-axis Motor](https://us.store.bambulab.com/collections/spare-parts-for-a1-series/products/x-motor-a1-mini) | This component is used to drive the tool head in the back-and-forth motion along the X-axis |
| 2. Spool Holder Bracket | This component is used to attach the [Spool Holder](https://us.store.bambulab.com/collections/spare-parts-for-a1-series/products/spool-holder-a1-mini) to the back of the A1 mini. |
| 3. [Nozzle Wiper](https://us.store.bambulab.com/collections/spare-parts-for-a1-series/products/heatbed-nozzle-wiper-a1-mini) | This component is used for cleaning the nozzle before starting a print job |
| 4. [Heatbed Cable](https://us.store.bambulab.com/collections/spare-parts-for-a1-series/products/heatbed-cable-a1-mini) | This component provides power to the [Heatbed](https://us.store.bambulab.com/collections/spare-parts-for-a1-series/products/a1-mini-heatbed-unit) and sensor feedback to the Motherboard. |
| 5. Eddy Sensor | This component is used during calibration of the Heatbed. |
| 6. Power Switch | This component is used to turn the printer on and off |
| 7. [Toolhead Board](https://us.store.bambulab.com/collections/spare-parts-for-a1-series/products/toolhead-board-a1-series) | This component is the main circuit board of the toolhead which provides connectivity to the toolhead parts |
| 8. Extruder Motor | This is the motor attached to the Extruder Unit which provides motion to the [Extruder Gear Assembly](https://us.store.bambulab.com/products/hardened-steel-extruder-gear-assembly-a1-series) |
| 9. [X-Axis Belt](https://us.store.bambulab.com/products/x-belt-a1-mini) |
| This component is the transmission component for the X-axis assembly to provide motion in the X direction. |
| 10. Purge Wiper | This component helps with removing the purged filament before and during printing, to remove the purged material. |
| 11. [Y-axis Motor](https://us.store.bambulab.com/collections/spare-parts-for-a1-series/products/y-motor-a1-mini) | This component is used to drive the heated bed in the back-and-forth motion along the Y-axis. |
| 12. [Y-axis Belt](https://us.store.bambulab.com/collections/spare-parts-for-a1-series/products/y-belt-a1-mini) | This component is the transmission component for the Y-axis assembly to provide motion in the Y direction. |

## [¶](#toolhead-view) Toolhead View

![](/glossary/a1_series_glossary_-_toolhead.jpg)

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| 1. Extruder Idler Lever | This component is used for apply pressure against the idler of the extruder to help with extrusion. It is part of the [extruder unit](https://us.store.bambulab.com/products/extruder-unit-a1-series). |
| 2. [Filament Sensor](https://us.store.bambulab.com/products/filament-sensor-a1-series) | This component is located inside the [Filament Hub](https://us.store.bambulab.com/products/ams-lite-filament-hub) and detects the movement and presence of filament. |
| 3. [Extruder Unit](https://us.store.bambulab.com/products/extruder-unit-a1-series) | This is the component which control the movement of the filament and pushes it through the [Hotend](https://us.store.bambulab.com/products/hotend-with-0-4-mm-nozzle-a1-series). |
| 4. [Hotend Fan](https://us.store.bambulab.com/products/hotend-cooling-fan-a1-series) | This component is used to cool down both the [hotend](https://us.store.bambulab.com/products/hotend-with-0-4-mm-nozzle-a1-series) and [heating assembly](https://us.store.bambulab.com/products/hotend-heating-assembly-a1-series). |
| 5. [Hotend](https://us.store.bambulab.com/products/hotend-with-0-4-mm-nozzle-a1-series) | This component is melting the filament before the printer lays down the layer lines of the 3D printed model. |
| 6. [USB Cable](https://us.store.bambulab.com/collections/spare-parts-for-a1-series/products/bambu-usb-c-cable-a1-mini) | This component connects the Toolhead to the Mainboard. |
| 7. Filament Hub | This component is used for connecting all the PTFE tubes to the Toolhead. It also contains the [filament sensor](https://us.store.bambulab.com/products/filament-sensor-a1-series). |
| 8. Extruder Motor | This is the motor attached to the Extruder Unit which provides motion to the [Extruder Gear Assembly](https://us.store.bambulab.com/products/hardened-steel-extruder-gear-assembly-a1-series) |
| 9. [X-Axis Belt Tensioner](https://us.store.bambulab.com/products/x-belt-a1-mini) |
| This component is the transmission component for the X-axis assembly to provide motion in the X direction. |
| 10. [Filament Cutter Lever](https://us.store.bambulab.com/products/filament-cutter-lever-a1-series) | This component provides the mechanical cutting force to the Filament Cutting Blade. |
| 11. [Hotend Heating Assembly](https://us.store.bambulab.com/products/hotend-heating-assembly-a1-series) | This component provides heat to the Hotend while also enabling quick swap for easy replacement. |
| 12. [Part Cooling Fan](https://us.store.bambulab.com/collections/spare-parts-for-a1-series/products/part-cooling-fan-a1-series) | This component cools the printed layer lines to solidify the filament quickly. |

A1 mini Packing Guide

This guide explains how to pack A1 mini.

---

## [¶](#packing-tips) Packing Tips

Proper packaging is the most effective way to avoid shipping damage, as it ensures the safety of your goods and minimizes unnecessary economic losses.

> **To ensure your equipment remains secure during transit, we kindly remind you to:**
>
> * **Check that all screws are tightened**, to secure internal components.
> * **Check that all cushioning foam is properly placed**, to fully protect the equipment.

## [¶](#video-guide) Video Guide

## [¶](#operation-guide) Operation Guide

### [¶](#unload-the-filament) Unload the filament

Step 1. Tap the **Filament** on the screen.

![filament.png](/a1m/manual/a1-mini-packing/filament.png)

Step 2. Tap the **Unload**.

![unload.png](/a1m/manual/a1-mini-packing/unload.png)

Step 3. Unload the filament and remove the spool.

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![](/a1m/manual/a1-mini-packing/%E9%80%80%E5%87%BA%E8%80%97%E6%9D%90.png) | ![](/a1m/manual/a1-mini-packing/%E5%8F%96%E4%B8%8B%E6%96%99%E7%9B%98.png) |

### [¶](#remove-the-tube) Remove the tube

Step 1. Remove the cable organizer

![取下集线器-.png](/a1m/manual/a1-mini-packing/%E5%8F%96%E4%B8%8B%E9%9B%86%E7%BA%BF%E5%99%A8-.png)

Step 2. Press (by tweezers) to disconnect the tube.

![取下料管-.png](/a1m/manual/a1-mini-packing/%E5%8F%96%E4%B8%8B%E6%96%99%E7%AE%A1-.png)

Step 3. Press the filament connector to disconnect the tube.

![按压料管接口.png](/a1m/manual/a1-mini-packing/%E6%8C%89%E5%8E%8B%E6%96%99%E7%AE%A1%E6%8E%A5%E5%8F%A3.png)

Step 4. Put the tube back.

![料管入袋.png](/a1m/manual/a1-mini-packing/%E6%96%99%E7%AE%A1%E5%85%A5%E8%A2%8B.png)

### [¶](#power-off-the-printer) Power off the printer

Power off the printer and disconnect the power cable.

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![](/a1m/manual/a1-mini-packing/%E5%85%B3%E9%97%AD%E7%94%B5%E6%BA%90-.png) | ![](/a1m/manual/ai-mini-combo-pack/%E6%8B%94%E7%94%B5%E6%BA%90%E7%BA%BF.png) |

### [¶](#remove-the-spool-holder-and-purge-wiper) Remove the spool holder and purge wiper

Step 1. Slide the spool holder upwards to remove it.

![向上滑出料盘支架.png](/a1m/manual/ai-mini-combo-pack/%E5%90%91%E4%B8%8A%E6%BB%91%E5%87%BA%E6%96%99%E7%9B%98%E6%94%AF%E6%9E%B6.png)

Step 2. Remove the 2 screws to remove the spool holder bracket.

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![](/a1m/manual/ai-mini-combo-pack/%E7%A7%BB%E9%99%A4%E4%B8%A4%E9%A2%97%E6%96%99%E7%9B%98%E6%94%AF%E6%9E%B6%E5%BA%95%E5%BA%A7%E8%9E%BA%E4%B8%9D.png) | ![](/a1m/manual/ai-mini-combo-pack/%E7%A7%BB%E9%99%A4%E6%96%99%E7%9B%98%E6%94%AF%E6%9E%B6%E5%BA%95%E5%BA%A7.png) |

Step 3. Remove the screw under the purge wiper.

![吐料组件螺丝.png](/a1m/manual/ai-mini-combo-pack/%E5%90%90%E6%96%99%E7%BB%84%E4%BB%B6%E8%9E%BA%E4%B8%9D.png)

Step 4. Slide the purge wiper.

![左滑吐料组件.png](/a1m/manual/ai-mini-combo-pack/%E5%B7%A6%E6%BB%91%E5%90%90%E6%96%99%E7%BB%84%E4%BB%B6.png)

### [¶](#fix-the-toolhead-x-axis-heatbed-and-z-axis) Fix the toolhead, X-axis, heatbed and Z-axis

Step 1. Loosen the heatbed screws by two turns.

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![](/a1m/manual/ai-mini-combo-pack/%E6%9D%BE%E4%B8%A4%E5%9C%88%E7%83%AD%E5%BA%8A%E8%9E%BA%E4%B8%9D1.png) | ![](/a1m/manual/ai-mini-combo-pack/%E9%80%81%E4%B8%A4%E5%9C%88%E7%83%AD%E5%BA%8A%E8%9E%BA%E4%B8%9D2.png) |

Step 2. Place foam on the end and move the toolhead close to it.

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![](/a1m/manual/ai-mini-combo-pack/%E6%94%BE%E7%BD%AE%E5%B7%A5%E5%85%B7%E5%A4%B4%E6%B3%A1%E6%A3%89.png) | ![](/a1m/manual/ai-mini-combo-pack/%E5%B0%86%E5%B7%A5%E5%85%B7%E5%A4%B4%E7%A7%BB%E5%88%B0%E6%9C%AB%E7%AB%AF.png) |

Step 3. Secure the toolhead with a zip tie.

![用线扎固定工具头.png](/a1m/manual/ai-mini-combo-pack/%E7%94%A8%E7%BA%BF%E6%89%8E%E5%9B%BA%E5%AE%9A%E5%B7%A5%E5%85%B7%E5%A4%B4.png)

Step 4. Put on the X-axis protective cover and place the foam back.

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![](/a1m/manual/ai-mini-combo-pack/%E5%A5%97%E4%B8%8Ax%E8%BD%B4%E4%BF%9D%E6%8A%A4%E5%A5%97.png) | ![](/a1m/manual/ai-mini-combo-pack/%E6%94%BE%E7%BD%AEx%E8%BD%B4%E6%B3%A1%E6%A3%89.png) |

Step 5. Place 2 pieces of foam under the heatbed.

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![](/a1m/manual/ai-mini-combo-pack/%E6%94%BE%E7%BD%AE%E7%83%AD%E5%BA%8A%E6%B3%A1%E6%A3%891.png) | ![](/a1m/manual/ai-mini-combo-pack/%E6%94%BE%E7%BD%AE%E7%83%AD%E5%BA%8A%E6%B3%A1%E6%A3%892.png) |

Step 6. **Align the Z-axis limiter with the screw holes** and install the 4 screws.

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![](/a1m/manual/ai-mini-combo-pack/z%E8%BD%B4%E9%99%90%E5%88%B6%E5%99%A8%E8%9E%BA%E4%B8%9D%E5%AD%94.png) | ![](/a1m/manual/ai-mini-combo-pack/%E5%AE%89%E8%A3%854%E9%A2%97%E8%9E%BA%E4%B8%9D.png) |

### [¶](#packing-operation) Packing operation

Step 1. Roll up the cable.

![电源电缆.png](/a1m/manual/a1-mini-packing/%E7%94%B5%E6%BA%90%E7%94%B5%E7%BC%86.png)

Step 2. Place accessories at the bottom of the carton.

![纸箱底部.png](/a1m/manual/a1-mini-packing/%E7%BA%B8%E7%AE%B1%E5%BA%95%E9%83%A8.png)

Step 3. Put the printer back into the carton.

![打印机放回.png](/a1m/manual/a1-mini-packing/%E6%89%93%E5%8D%B0%E6%9C%BA%E6%94%BE%E5%9B%9E.png)

Step 4. Place the foam cardboard.

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![](/a1m/manual/a1-mini-packing/%E6%B3%A1%E6%A3%89.png) | ![](/a1m/manual/a1-mini-packing/%E6%B3%A1%E6%A3%892.png) |

Step 5. Place the printer's fixing cardboard and align it with the cardboard clips.

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![](/a1m/manual/a1-mini-packing/%E5%8D%A1%E6%89%A3.png) | ![](/a1m/manual/a1-mini-packing/%E6%89%A3%E7%B4%A7%E7%BA%B8%E7%AE%B1%E5%8D%A1%E6%89%A3.png) |

Step 6. Place the accessory box and Z-axis top cover foam.

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![](/a1m/manual/a1-mini-packing/%E9%85%8D%E4%BB%B6%E7%9B%92.png) | ![](/a1m/manual/a1-mini-packing/z%E8%BD%B4%E4%B8%8A%E7%9B%96%E6%B3%A1%E6%A3%89.png) |

Step 7. Place the top cardboard.

|  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- |
| ![](/a1m/manual/a1-mini-packing/%E7%BA%B8%E6%9D%BF1.png) | ![](/a1m/manual/a1-mini-packing/%E7%BA%B8%E6%9D%BF2.png) | ![](/a1m/manual/a1-mini-packing/%E7%BA%B8%E6%9D%BF3.png) |

Step 8. Cover the carton and secure it with tape.

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![](/a1m/manual/ai-mini-combo-pack/%E5%B0%81%E7%AE%B1.png) | ![](/a1m/manual/ai-mini-combo-pack/%E8%B4%B4%E8%83%B6%E5%B8%A6.png) |

## [¶](#end-notes) End Notes

A1 mini combo Packing Guide

This guide explains how to pack the A1 mini combo

---

## [¶](#packing-tips) Packing Tips

Proper packaging is the most effective way to avoid shipping damage, as it ensures the safety of your goods and minimizes unnecessary economic losses.

> **To ensure your equipment remains secure during transit, we kindly remind you to:**
>
> * **Check that all screws are tightened**, to secure internal components.
> * **Check that all cushioning foam is properly placed**, to fully protect the equipment.

## [¶](#video-guide) Video Guide

## [¶](#operation-guide) Operation Guide

### [¶](#power-off-the-printer) Power off the printer

Power off the printer and disconnect the power cable.

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![](/a1m/manual/ai-mini-combo-pack/%E5%85%B3%E9%97%AD%E7%94%B5%E6%BA%90.png) | ![](/a1m/manual/ai-mini-combo-pack/%E6%8B%94%E7%94%B5%E6%BA%90%E7%BA%BF.png) |

### [¶](#remove-the-spool-and-ptfe-tube) Remove the spool and PTFE tube

Step 1. Unload the filament from the AMS Lite.

![手动退料.png](/a1m/manual/ai-mini-combo-pack/%E6%89%8B%E5%8A%A8%E9%80%80%E6%96%99.png)

Step 2. Remove the spool from the rotary spool holder.

![拔出料盘.png](/a1m/manual/ai-mini-combo-pack/%E6%8B%94%E5%87%BA%E6%96%99%E7%9B%98.png)

Step 3. Disconnect the 4-pin cable.

![断开4pin线.png](/a1m/manual/ai-mini-combo-pack/%E6%96%AD%E5%BC%804pin%E7%BA%BF.png)

Step 4. Remove the cable organizer.

![取下集线器.png](/a1m/manual/ai-mini-combo-pack/%E5%8F%96%E4%B8%8B%E9%9B%86%E7%BA%BF%E5%99%A8.png)

Step 5. Press the toolhead filament inlet connector (by tweezers) to disconnect 4 PTFE tubes.

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![](/a1-combo-packing/36.png) | ![](/a1m/manual/ai-mini-combo-pack/%E9%95%8A%E5%AD%90-%E6%8B%94%E5%87%BA%E6%96%99%E7%AE%A1.png) |

Step 6. Press the filament connector in AMS Lite to disconnect 4 PTFE tubes.

![拔出ams_lite料管.png](/a1m/manual/ai-mini-combo-pack/%E6%8B%94%E5%87%BAams_lite%E6%96%99%E7%AE%A1.png)

### [¶](#remove-the-ams-lite) Remove the AMS Lite

Step 1. Pull out 4 rotary spool holders.

![拔下回转轴.png](/a1m/manual/ai-mini-combo-pack/%E6%8B%94%E4%B8%8B%E5%9B%9E%E8%BD%AC%E8%BD%B4.png)

Step 2. Remove the 4 screws connecting the AMS Lite body and the stand.

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![](/a1-combo-packing/41.png) | ![](/a1-combo-packing/42.png) |

Step 3. Remove the AMS Lite body.

![取下ams主体.png](/a1m/manual/ai-mini-combo-pack/%E5%8F%96%E4%B8%8Bams%E4%B8%BB%E4%BD%93.png)

Step 4. Fix the feeder unit with zip ties.

![线扎固定ams.png](/a1m/manual/ai-mini-combo-pack/%E7%BA%BF%E6%89%8E%E5%9B%BA%E5%AE%9Aams.png)

> Note: On some versions, there may be 3 zip ties here.
>  ![a1mini_combo扎带.jpg](/a1-combo-packing/a1mini_combo%E6%89%8E%E5%B8%A6.jpg)

Step 5. Put the PTFE tube back.

![放回料管.png](/a1m/manual/ai-mini-combo-pack/%E6%94%BE%E5%9B%9E%E6%96%99%E7%AE%A1.png)

### [¶](#remove-the-spool-holder-and-purge-wiper) Remove the spool holder and purge wiper

Step 1. Slide the spool holder upwards to remove it.

![向上滑出料盘支架.png](/a1m/manual/ai-mini-combo-pack/%E5%90%91%E4%B8%8A%E6%BB%91%E5%87%BA%E6%96%99%E7%9B%98%E6%94%AF%E6%9E%B6.png)

Step 2. Remove the 2 screws to remove the spool holder bracket.

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![](/a1m/manual/ai-mini-combo-pack/%E7%A7%BB%E9%99%A4%E4%B8%A4%E9%A2%97%E6%96%99%E7%9B%98%E6%94%AF%E6%9E%B6%E5%BA%95%E5%BA%A7%E8%9E%BA%E4%B8%9D.png) | ![](/a1m/manual/ai-mini-combo-pack/%E7%A7%BB%E9%99%A4%E6%96%99%E7%9B%98%E6%94%AF%E6%9E%B6%E5%BA%95%E5%BA%A7.png) |

Step 3. Remove the screw under the purge wiper.

![吐料组件螺丝.png](/a1m/manual/ai-mini-combo-pack/%E5%90%90%E6%96%99%E7%BB%84%E4%BB%B6%E8%9E%BA%E4%B8%9D.png)

Step 4. Slide the purge wiper.

![左滑吐料组件.png](/a1m/manual/ai-mini-combo-pack/%E5%B7%A6%E6%BB%91%E5%90%90%E6%96%99%E7%BB%84%E4%BB%B6.png)

### [¶](#fix-the-toolhead-x-axis-heatbed-and-z-axis) Fix the toolhead, X-axis, heatbed and Z-axis

Step 1. Loosen the heatbed screws by two turns.

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![](/a1m/manual/ai-mini-combo-pack/%E6%9D%BE%E4%B8%A4%E5%9C%88%E7%83%AD%E5%BA%8A%E8%9E%BA%E4%B8%9D1.png) | ![](/a1m/manual/ai-mini-combo-pack/%E9%80%81%E4%B8%A4%E5%9C%88%E7%83%AD%E5%BA%8A%E8%9E%BA%E4%B8%9D2.png) |

Step 2. Place foam on the end and move the toolhead close to it.

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![](/a1m/manual/ai-mini-combo-pack/%E6%94%BE%E7%BD%AE%E5%B7%A5%E5%85%B7%E5%A4%B4%E6%B3%A1%E6%A3%89.png) | ![](/a1m/manual/ai-mini-combo-pack/%E5%B0%86%E5%B7%A5%E5%85%B7%E5%A4%B4%E7%A7%BB%E5%88%B0%E6%9C%AB%E7%AB%AF.png) |

Step 3. Secure the toolhead with a zip tie.

![用线扎固定工具头.png](/a1m/manual/ai-mini-combo-pack/%E7%94%A8%E7%BA%BF%E6%89%8E%E5%9B%BA%E5%AE%9A%E5%B7%A5%E5%85%B7%E5%A4%B4.png)

Step 4. Put on the X-axis protective cover and place the foam back.

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![](/a1m/manual/ai-mini-combo-pack/%E5%A5%97%E4%B8%8Ax%E8%BD%B4%E4%BF%9D%E6%8A%A4%E5%A5%97.png) | ![](/a1m/manual/ai-mini-combo-pack/%E6%94%BE%E7%BD%AEx%E8%BD%B4%E6%B3%A1%E6%A3%89.png) |

Step 5. Place 2 pieces of foam under the heatbed.

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![](/a1m/manual/ai-mini-combo-pack/%E6%94%BE%E7%BD%AE%E7%83%AD%E5%BA%8A%E6%B3%A1%E6%A3%891.png) | ![](/a1-combo-packing/%E5%B7%A6%E4%BE%A7%E6%B3%A1%E6%A3%89.png) |
| ![](/a1m/manual/ai-mini-combo-pack/%E6%94%BE%E7%BD%AE%E7%83%AD%E5%BA%8A%E6%B3%A1%E6%A3%891.png) | ![](/a1-combo-packing/%E5%8F%B3%E4%BE%A7%E6%B3%A1%E6%A3%89.png) |

Step 6. **Align the Z-axis limiter with the screw holes** and install the 4 screws.

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![](/a1m/manual/ai-mini-combo-pack/z%E8%BD%B4%E9%99%90%E5%88%B6%E5%99%A8%E8%9E%BA%E4%B8%9D%E5%AD%94.png) | ![](/a1m/manual/ai-mini-combo-pack/%E5%AE%89%E8%A3%854%E9%A2%97%E8%9E%BA%E4%B8%9D.png) |

### [¶](#put-back-the-accessories) Put back the accessories

Step 1. Put the accessory box into the foam box.

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![](/a1m/manual/ai-mini-combo-pack/%E6%94%BE%E5%9B%9E%E9%85%8D%E4%BB%B61.png) | ![](/a1m/manual/ai-mini-combo-pack/%E6%94%BE%E5%9B%9E%E9%85%8D%E4%BB%B62.png) |

> Filament swatches are no longer included in the latest packaging of A1 series.

Step 2. Put back the rotary spool holders.

![放回回转轴.png](/a1m/manual/ai-mini-combo-pack/%E6%94%BE%E5%9B%9E%E5%9B%9E%E8%BD%AC%E8%BD%B4.png)

Step 3. Place the AMS Lite body foam.

![装上ams主体泡棉.png](/a1m/manual/ai-mini-combo-pack/%E8%A3%85%E4%B8%8Aams%E4%B8%BB%E4%BD%93%E6%B3%A1%E6%A3%89.png)

> Note: On some versions, there may be 3 zip ties here.
>  ![a1mini_combo扎带.jpg](/a1-combo-packing/a1mini_combo%E6%89%8E%E5%B8%A6.jpg)

### [¶](#packing-operation) Packing operation

Step 1. Place some accessories such as PTFE tubes and instruction at the bottom of the carton.

![料管、说明书等放入底部.png](/a1m/manual/ai-mini-combo-pack/%E6%96%99%E7%AE%A1%E3%80%81%E8%AF%B4%E6%98%8E%E4%B9%A6%E7%AD%89%E6%94%BE%E5%85%A5%E5%BA%95%E9%83%A8.png)

Step 2. Put the printer back into the carton.

![放入打印机.png](/a1m/manual/ai-mini-combo-pack/%E6%94%BE%E5%85%A5%E6%89%93%E5%8D%B0%E6%9C%BA.png)

Step 3. Place the foam cardboard.

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![](/a1m/manual/ai-mini-combo-pack/%E6%94%BE%E5%85%A5%E6%B3%A1%E6%A3%89%E7%BA%B8%E6%9D%BF.png) | ![](/a1m/manual/ai-mini-combo-pack/%E6%94%BE%E5%85%A5_%E6%B3%A1%E6%A3%89%E7%BA%B8%E6%9D%BF2.png) |

Step 4. Place the AMS Lite holder and foam cardboard.

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![](/a1m/manual/ai-mini-combo-pack/%E6%94%BE%E5%85%A5ams%E5%BA%95%E5%BA%A7.png) | ![](/a1m/manual/ai-mini-combo-pack/%E5%92%8Cams%E6%B3%A1%E6%A3%89.png) |

Step 5. Place the AMS Lite body and accessory box.

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![](/a1m/manual/ai-mini-combo-pack/%E6%94%BE%E5%85%A5ams%E4%B8%BB%E4%BD%93.png) | ![](/a1m/manual/ai-mini-combo-pack/%E4%B8%BB%E4%BD%93%E6%B3%A1%E6%A3%89.png) |

Step 6. Close the carton after covering it with cardboard and secure it with tape.

|  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- |
| ![](/a1m/manual/ai-mini-combo-pack/%E7%9B%96%E4%B8%8A%E7%BA%B8%E6%9D%BF.png) | ![](/a1m/manual/ai-mini-combo-pack/%E5%B0%81%E7%AE%B1.png) | ![](/a1m/manual/ai-mini-combo-pack/%E8%B4%B4%E8%83%B6%E5%B8%A6.png) |

## [¶](#end-notes) End Notes

> We hope the detailed guide provided has been helpful and informative.

Please include a picture or video illustrating the problem, as well as any additional information related to your inquiry.

A1 mini and A1 mini combo unboxing guide

Steps and precautions for unboxing A1 mini and A1 mini combo

---

We recommend that you watch the video first before unboxing to ensure the operations are correct.

Slight differences may be found between your printer and the one shown in the unboxing video in terms of protective films, foams, and cardboard.

The screen is covered with one or two protective films to prevent contamination during production and transportation. For two protective films, there will be a tear on the first film for you to tear it off. If there is only one protective film, there will be no tear. Either of these two situations is normal for a new printer.

If your printer screen has two protective films, you can tear off the first one. If there is only one film, you can decide whether to keep it or not. However, we recommend that you keep the one film to avoid scratches during use.

**Filament swatches are no longer included in the latest packaging of A1 series. The items in the tool box are placed inside transparent plastic bags.**

## [¶](#important-reminder) Important Reminder:

The Y-axis of the A1 mini utilizes full-metal guide wheels, ensuring that users do not need to frequently adjust the motion components of the A1 mini. However, regular lubrication and cleaning are required to reduce wear and noise, maintain printer accuracy and lifespan, and effectively prevent rusting of the guide rails.

Each printer receives a certain level of lubrication before leaving the factory. However, only a small amount of lubricating oil is applied to the printer to avoid excessive oil that may drip onto the casing during transportation, causing noticeable stains and affecting the appearance.

Therefore, we strongly recommend applying an appropriate amount of lubricating oil to the Y-axis guide rails of your printer upon unboxing, ensuring that the entire rail is lubricated.

The lubricating oil is included in the accessory box. Please refer to the accompanying video for instructions on how to apply the lubricant:

[Click here to watch the video](https://public-cdn.bblmw.com/wiki/video/lubricate-rails.mp4)

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![](/n1/manual/unboxing/smooth-y.jpg)  Picture of the Y-axis guide rails after proper lubrication. | ![](/n1/manual/unboxing/%E6%B6%A6%E6%BB%91%E6%B2%B9en.jpg) |

## [¶](#steps-to-unbox-and-take-out-the-a1-mini-ams-lite-and-their-accessories) Steps to unbox and take out the A1 mini, AMS lite, and their accessories

**The protective foam may vary from the machine shown in the unboxing video. Please refer to the machine you have received for accurate information. For example, some machines may have protective foam installed beneath the heatbed, which needs to be removed during the unboxing process. Early boxes include Bambu Filament Swatches when unboxing, while the latest packaging does not include Bambu Filament Swatches in the box.**

The screen surface is covered with one or two layers of protective film, the purpose of which is to prevent contamination during production and transportation. Regardless of the state you receive, it is a normal new printer.

Some printers are manufactured with two layers of protective film (the first layer has a tear-off position), while others have only one layer (if you don't see a tear-off position, it means there is only one layer). Both situations will be shipped simultaneously, and you should refer to the actual machine you receive.

If your printer screen has two film layers, you can peel off the outer layer. If there is only one layer, you can decide whether to peel off this layer or not, you can peel it or not. However, we recommend that you keep the last one so that you can avoid scratches when using the screen.

### [¶](#h-1-remove-the-protective-cardboard-from-the-top) 1. Remove the protective cardboard from the top

**Some accessories may be embedded in the foam, so please check them carefully. Please take out the components one by one and avoid removing the plastic packaging bags from the box all at once.**

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![](/a1m/manual/unbox/unbox_(1).png) | ![](/a1m/manual/unbox/unbox_(2).png) |

### [¶](#h-2-carefully-read-the-quick-start-guide-and-familiarize-yourself-with-the-precautions-for-unboxing-in-advance) **2. Carefully read the quick start guide and familiarize yourself with the precautions for unboxing in advance.**

Take out the accessories, AMS lite, and the printer. **When picking up the printer, support the rear cover of the Z-axis with your hand and avoid touching the metal rails of the Z-axis and X-axis with your hands.**

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![](/a1m/manual/unbox/unbox_(3).png) | ![](/a1m/manual/unbox/unbox_(4).png) |
| ![](/a1m/manual/unbox/unbox_(5).png) | ![](/a1m/manual/unbox/unbox_(6).png) |
| ![](/a1m/manual/unbox/unbox_(7).png) |  |

## [¶](#check-the-accessories-in-the-box) Check the accessories in the box

**With AMS lite**: The packaging box includes the following items: A1 mini printer, AMS lite body（including 4-pin cable）, AMS lite stand and installation screws, AMS lite Rotary spool holder, purge wiper and its screws, spool holder with the bracket and its screws, PTFE tubes (580 mm \*2, 700 mm \*2), textured pei plate, sample filament, unclogging pin tool, allen keys (H2 and H1.5), 32 GB Micro SD card (pre-installed in the printer), heatbed nozzle wiper, cable organizer, bambu scraper blade and its screws, spare filament cutter, lubricating oil and grease (2 types of packages randomly shipped), quick start guide, and disclaimer and safety guidelines.

**Without AMS lite**: The packaging box includes the following items: A1 mini printer, purge wiper and its screws, spool holder with the bracket and its screws, PTFE tube (520 mm \*1), Textured PEI plate, sample filament, unclogging pin tool, allen keys (H2 and H1.5), 32 GB Micro SD card (pre-installed in the printer), heatbed nozzle wiper, cable organizer, bambu scraper blade and its screws, spare filament cutter, lubricating oil and grease (2 types of packages randomly shipped), quick start guide, and disclaimer and safety guidelines.

| **With AMS lite** | **Without AMS lite** |

### [¶](#h-1-remove-the-4-screws-to-unlock-the-z-axis-limiter-that-is-used-to-protect-the-printer-during-transportation) 1. Remove the 4 screws to unlock the Z-axis limiter that is used to protect the printer during transportation.

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![](/n1/manual/unboxing-a1-mini-urey/unlock-z1.png) | ![](/n1/manual/unboxing-a1-mini-urey/unlock-z2.png) |

### [¶](#h-2-cut-the-zip-tie-wrapped-around-the-tool-head) 2. Cut the zip-tie wrapped around the tool head.

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![](/n1/manual/unboxing-a1-mini-urey/unlock-x1.png) | ![](/n1/manual/unboxing-a1-mini-urey/unlock-x2.png) |

### [¶](#h-3-move-the-tool-head-slightly-to-the-left-and-then-remove-the-foam-padding) 3. Move the tool head slightly to the left and then remove the foam padding.

![](/n1/manual/unboxing-a1-mini-urey/unlock-x3.png)

![](https://wiki.bambulab.com/n1/manual/unboxing/remove-foam.gif)

If there is protective foam around the heatbed and guide rails, it should be removed at this step.

## [¶](#tighten-the-heatbed-screws) Tighten the heatbed screws.

To prevent damage to parts due to impact during transportation, the 3 screws at the bottom of the heatbed (as shown in the green mark in the picture) will be loosened during the factory assembly process. So you need to tighten them.

![](/n1/manual/unboxing-a1-mini-urey/lock-bed1.png)

It is recommended to push the heatbed slightly to a position where you can use the Allen key, insert the key first, and then fix the key and the build plate with your hands to prevent the screws from falling off.

![](/n1/manual/unboxing-a1-mini-urey/lock-bed2.png)

![](/a1m/manual/unbox/%E7%94%A8%E6%89%8B%E5%9B%BA%E5%AE%9A.jpg)

## [¶](#install-the-purge-wiper) Install the purge wiper

Slide in the Purge Wiper unit into the slot at the end of the X-Axis from the back to the front. Install the 1\*M3-12 screw (for purge wiper) from the accessory box to fix the Purge Wiper in place.

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![](/n1/manual/unboxing-a1-mini-urey/purge1.png) | ![](/a1m/manual/unbox/unbox_(13).png) |
| ![](/n1/manual/unboxing-a1-mini-urey/purge3.png) | ![](/n1/manual/unboxing-a1-mini-urey/purge4.png) |

**Note: After installing the purge wiper, it should be parallel to and not collide with the left edge of the heatbed, as shown in the picture below:**

![](https://wiki.bambulab.com/n1/manual/unboxing-a1-mini-urey/purge5v.png)

## [¶](#steps-to-install-the-spool-holder) Steps to install the spool holder

### [¶](#h-1-install-the-spool-holder-base-plate-with-the-2m3-8-screws-for-spool-holder-from-the-accessory-box-please-pay-attention-to-the-installation-orientation) 1. Install the spool holder base plate with the 2\*M3-8 screws (for spool holder) from the accessory box.  Please pay attention to the installation orientation.

If you want to print with AMS lite, this step would be optional.

Slide the rotary spool holders all the way in. Be careful to match colors to avoid damaging any parts (as shown in the diagram, yellow matches with yellow, green matches with green).

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![](/n1/manual/unboxing-a1-mini-urey/assembly-ams5.png) | ![](/n1/manual/unboxing-a1-mini-urey/assembly-ams6-en.png) |

The yellow rotary spool holders have a hexagonal clip inside, making aligning with the locking position relatively easier. The green rotary spool holders have a rectangular shape, so it is recommended to align them with the hole before insertion roughly, then move them parallelly with slight adjustments to ensure alignment with the locking position.

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![](/n1/manual/unboxing-a1-mini-urey/assembly-ams7.png) | ![](/n1/manual/unboxing-a1-mini-urey/assembly-ams8.png) |

### [¶](#h-4-place-the-ams-lite-on-the-right-side-of-the-a1-mini-printer-after-successfully-installing-the-rotary-spool-holders) 4. Place the AMS lite on the right side of the A1 mini printer after successfully installing the rotary spool holders.

![](/n1/manual/unboxing-a1-mini-urey/assembly-ams9.png)

### [¶](#h-5-install-ptfe-tubes) 5. Install PTFE tubes

As the pictures show, insert the 580 mm PTFE tubes into feeder coupler connectors 1 and 2. Insert the other end of the PTFE tube from port 1 into the left-rear side of the filament hub, and insert the other end of the PTFE tube from port 2 into the left-front side of the filament hub. (Viewed from the front to the back)

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![](/a1m/manual/unbox/unbox_(18).png) | ![](/a1m/manual/unbox/unbox_(19).png) |

As the pictures show, insert the 700 mm PTFE tubes into feeder coupler connectors 3 and 4. Insert the other end of the PTFE tube from port 3 into the right-front side of the filament hub, and insert the other end of the PTFE tube from port 4 into the right-rear side of the filament hub. (Viewed from the front to the back)

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![](/a1m/manual/unbox/unbox_(20).png) | ![](/a1m/manual/unbox/unbox_(21).png) |

Note: If you have mistakenly inserted the PTFE tube during the installation process, please refer this Wiki to remove it: [A1 series Printer PTFE Tube Removement](/en/a1-mini/maintenance/ptfe-tube)

![](/a1m/manual/unbox/unbox_(17).png)

## [¶](#install-the-cable-organizer) Install the cable organizer

Install the organizer as shown in the diagram. Clip the black cable into the smaller hole and secure the PTFE tubes into the other four larger holes. Please note that the cable organizer should be positioned close to the end of the tool head instead of the AMS lite.

> Failure to install the organizer may cause the cables to sag and scrape against the heatbed.
