---

Guide on how to lubricate the A1 mini Y-axis guide rail

---

## [¶](#introduction) Introduction

The Y-axis of the A1 mini utilizes full-metal guide wheels, ensuring that users do not need to adjust the motion components of the A1 mini frequently. However, regular lubrication and cleaning are required to reduce wear and noise, maintain printer accuracy and lifespan, and effectively prevent rusting of the guide rails.

Each printer receives a certain level of lubrication before leaving the factory. However, only a small amount of lubricating oil is applied to the printer to avoid excessive oil that may drip onto the casing during transportation, causing noticeable stains and affecting the appearance.

Therefore, we strongly recommend applying an appropriate amount of lubricating oil to the Y-axis guide rails of your printer upon unboxing, ensuring that the entire rail is lubricated.

In daily use, we recommend lubricating the Y-axis guide rail once a month. Additionally, when you receive a lubrication prompt, please follow the instructions to lubricate the Y-axis guide rail.

## [¶](#lubricant-oil-recommended) Lubricant oil recommended

Please use the lubricant oil from the accessory box and avoid lubricant grease. Please verify the label on the lubricant oil bottle: "lubricant oil." Some batches may have black fonts and patterns on the packaging.

If lubricating grease is accidentally used, please immediately remove it and reapply the provided lubricating oil from the accessory box.

![](/n1/maintenance/y-lubrication/lubricant-oil.png)

Besides the lubricating oil in the accessory box, you can also use "WD-40 Multi-Use Product" (both in large and small bottles, which are okay) to lubricate the Y-axis guide rails. ([Click here to be redirected to the recommended purchase link.](https://www.amazon.com/dp/B00JIHTCNM/ref%3Dsr_1_9?__mk_zh_CN=%E4%BA%9A%E9%A9%AC%E9%80%8A%E7%BD%91%E7%AB%99&crid=DP2TFICUC166&keywords=wd40&qid=1699844622&sprefix=wd4%2Caps%2C425&sr=8-9&th=1&language=en_US&currency=USD) )

* [How to lubricate the A1 series Y-axis guide rail (with "WD-40 Multi-Use Product") Take the A1 mini as an example](/en/a1-mini/maintenance/lubricate-y-axis-with-wd-40-multi-use-product)

**Please purchase the authentic "WD-40 Multi-Use Product".**

![](/n1/maintenance/y-lubrication/wd-40-en.png)

**We have not tested other types of lubricant oil and cannot determine if they may adversely affect the Y-axis slide rail (including but not limited to corrosion and wear of bearings and steel shafts).**

## [¶](#guide-for-lubrication-operations) Guide for Lubrication Operations

### [¶](#h-1-the-lubricant-oil-is-included-in-the-accessory-box-please-refer-to-the-video-for-instructions-on-how-to-apply-the-lubricant-oil) 1. The lubricant oil is included in the accessory box. Please refer to the video for instructions on how to apply the lubricant oil:

**Attention**: Please raise the toolhead to prevent it from scraping against the heatbed before the lubricate action.

![](/a1/maintenance/y-axis/lift_z_axis.jpg)

Raise the toolhead

### [¶](#h-2-please-refer-to-the-instructions-on-how-to-lubricate-the-y-axis-guide-rails-using-wd-40-multi-use-product) 2. Please refer to the instructions on how to lubricate the Y-axis guide rails using "WD-40 Multi-Use Product":

**Here are the guidelines for lubricating the Y-axis guide rails with "WD-40 Multi-Use Product":**

**1.  Raise the toolhead to prevent it from scraping against the heated bed.**

![](/a1/maintenance/y-axis/lift_z_axis.jpg)

Raise the toolhead

**2. To avoid small fibers on the guide rails, use a non-woven cloth for lubrication.**

**3. Before lubricating, use a clean, non-woven cloth to wipe off dust on the guide rails.**

**4. After wiping off the dust, spray WD-40 onto another clean, non-woven cloth, and then gently apply it to the Y-axis guide rail using the cloth with WD-40 Multi-Use Product.**

## [¶](#pictures-after-lubrication) Pictures after Lubrication

![](https://wiki.bambulab.com/n1/manual/unboxing/smooth-y.jpg)

Picture of the Y-axis guide rails after proper lubrication.

---

How to lubricate the A1 series with WD-40 Multi-Use Product

Guide showing how to lubricate the A1 series Y axis rail

---

## [¶](#introduction) Introduction

The Y-axis of the A1 series printer utilizes a full-metal guide wheel, and this guide rail system ensures that users do not need to frequently adjust the motion components of the A1. However, regular lubrication and cleaning are required to reduce wear and noise, ensure the accuracy and lifespan of the printer, and effectively prevent rusting of the guide rail. Each printer receives a certain degree of lubrication before leaving the factory, but only a small amount of lubricant is applied to the machine. This is because if an excessive amount of lubricant is applied, it may drip onto the casing during transportation, affecting the appearance.

Therefore, we strongly recommend that you check the lubrication of the Y-axis after unboxing the printer to ensure that the lubricant covers the entire guide rail evenly. If insufficient lubrication is found, please refer to the following A1 Y-axis lubrication guide.

* [Guide on how to lubricate the A1 Y-axis guide rail](/en/a1/maintenance/lubricate-y-axis) (with included lubricant oil)
* [Guide on how to lubricate the A1 mini Y-axis guide rail](/en/a1-mini/maintenance/lubricate-y-axis) (with included lubricant oil)

In daily use, we recommend lubricating the Y-axis guide rail once a month. Additionally, when you receive a lubrication prompt, please follow the instructions to lubricate the Y-axis guide rail.

In addition to the lubricant oil in the accessory box, you can use "WD-40 Multi-Use Product" (both large and small bottles are suitable) to lubricate the X-axis linear rail, Y-axis guide rail, and Z-axis lead screw of the A1 series. This video demonstrates how to effectively use "WD-40 Multi-Use Product" to lubricate the Y-axis guide rails of the A1 mini printer, ensuring that the lubricant does not splash around.

**Please purchase the authentic "WD-40 Multi-Use Product".** **Other types of WD-40 are not recommended to be used on the A1.**

[(Click here to be redirected to the recommended product purchase link)](https://amzn.to/47yTdGt)

![](https://wiki.bambulab.com/n1/maintenance/y-lubrication/wd-40-en.png)

**Important Note**
WD-40 is a Brand name, and doesn't necessarly reffer to a specific product type. We recommend the “WD-40 Multi-Use Product” as it has been tested extensively with the A1 and confirmed to be working as expected.
**We have not tested other types of lubricant oil and cannot determine if they may adversely affect the Y-axis slide rail (including but not limited to corrosion and wear of bearings and steel shafts).**
**Avoid using a different lubrication method, as it can lead to problems.**

During the operation, please handle with care and ensure that lubricant does not come into contact with the surface of the textured plate.
It is recommended to remove the textured plate from the heatbed during the operation to avoid any contamination, as it will lead to adhesion issues.
Always wash the plate with hot water and detergent if you encounter adhesion problems.

## [¶](#materials-required) Materials Required:

* Non-woven fabric
* [WD-40 Multi-Use Product](https://amzn.to/47yTdGt)

## [¶](#guide-for-applying-lubricant) Guide for Applying Lubricant

Video to be added

### [¶](#step-1-clean-guide-rails-with-a-non-woven-cloth-avoiding-fibrous-tissues) Step 1: Clean guide rails with a non-woven cloth, avoiding fibrous tissues.

![](/a1/manual/lubricate-y-with-wd-40/%E5%89%8D%E6%93%A6%E7%81%B0.png)

### [¶](#step-2-spray-evenly-shaken-wd-40-multi-use-product-onto-a-clean-non-woven-cloth) Step 2: Spray evenly shaken “WD-40 Multi-Use Product” onto a clean non-woven cloth.

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![](/a1/manual/lubricate-y-with-wd-40/%E5%96%B7%E8%87%B3%E6%97%A0%E7%BA%BA%E5%B8%831.png) | ![](/a1/manual/lubricate-y-with-wd-40/%E5%96%B7%E8%87%B3%E6%97%A0%E7%BA%BA%E5%B8%832.png) |

### [¶](#step-3-gently-apply-the-wd-40-dampened-cloth-to-the-y-axis-guide-rail) Step 3: Gently apply the WD-40-dampened cloth to the Y-axis guide rail.

![](/a1/manual/lubricate-y-with-wd-40/%E8%BD%BB%E6%8B%8D1.png)

### [¶](#step-4-move-the-heatbed-back-and-forth-to-evenly-distribute-the-wd-40-multi-use-product) Step 4: Move the heatbed back and forth to evenly distribute the "WD-40 Multi-Use Product".

![](/a1/manual/lubricate-y-with-wd-40/%E5%89%8D%E5%90%8E%E6%8E%A8%E5%8A%A8.png)

Following the same method, apply "WD-40 Multi-Use Product" to the remaining sections of the steel shaft.

|  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- |
| ![](/a1/manual/lubricate-y-with-wd-40/%E8%BD%BB%E6%8B%8D2.png) | ![](/a1/manual/lubricate-y-with-wd-40/%E8%BD%BB%E6%8B%8D3.png) | ![](/a1/manual/lubricate-y-with-wd-40/%E8%BD%BB%E6%8B%8D4.png) |

### [¶](#step-5-use-a-clean-non-woven-cloth-to-carefully-remove-any-oil-stains-from-the-surface-of-the-casing-and-guide-rail) Step 5: Use a clean non-woven cloth to carefully remove any oil stains from the surface of the casing and guide rail.

> **NOTE**: Be cautious not to wipe "WD-40 Multi-Use Product" off the steel shaft.

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![](/a1/manual/lubricate-y-with-wd-40/%E6%93%A6%E6%B2%B9%E6%B8%8D1.png) | ![](/a1/manual/lubricate-y-with-wd-40/%E6%93%A6%E6%B2%B9%E6%B8%8D2.png) |

A1 mini Bottom Base Installation Tutorial

This article provides guidelines and precautions for the disassembly and assembly of the Base Housing

## [¶](#previous-operation) Previous Operation

If needed, please refer to the [A1 mini Installation Tutorial](/en/a1-mini/maintenance/a1-mini-com) for the related previous operations.

## [¶](#safety-warning) Safety Warning

> **IMPORTANT!**
>
> It's crucial to **power off the printer before performing any maintenance work** on the printer and its electronics, including tool head wires, because leaving the printer on while conducting such tasks can cause a short circuit, which can lead to additional electronic damage and safety hazards.
>
> When you perform maintenane or troubleshooting on the printer, you may be required to disassemble some parts, including the hotend. This process can expose wires and electrical components that could potentially short circuit if they come into contact with each other or with other metal or electronic components while the printer is still on. **This can damage the electronics of the printer and cause further damage.**
>
> Therefore, it's essential to **switch off the printer and disconnect it from the power source before doing any maintenance work**. This will prevent any short circuits or damage to the printer's electronics. By doing so, you can avoid potential damage to the printer's electronic components and ensure that the maintenance work is performed safely and effectively.
>
> If you have any concerns or questions about following this guide, [open a new ticket in our Support Page](https://bambulab.com/en/my/support/tickets?from=5) and we will do our best to respond promptly and provide you with the assistance you need.

## [¶](#video-guide) Video Guide

Due to differences in product versions, there may be some differences in details.

## [¶](#disassemble-the-base-housing) Disassemble the Base Housing

### [¶](#step-1-remove-the-touch-screen-and-wifi-antenna) Step 1 - Remove the Touch Screen and Wifi Antenna

Unlock and disconnect the touch screen cable connection, and push outwards on the back of the display to loosen and remove the touch screen.

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![](/n1/a1-mini-base-housing/%E7%A7%BB%E9%99%A4%E8%A7%A6%E6%91%B8%E5%B1%8Ffpc.png) | ![](/n1/a1-mini-base-housing/%E7%A7%BB%E9%99%A4%E8%A7%A6%E6%91%B8%E5%B1%8F.png) |

Disconnect the antenna connection from the Mainboard and remove the antenna by prying up the WIFI antenna board with flat-tipped tweezers.

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![](/n1/a1-mini-base-housing/%E7%A7%BB%E9%99%A4wifi%E5%A4%A9%E7%BA%BF.png) | ![](/n1/a1-mini-base-housing/%E7%A7%BB%E9%99%A4wifi%E5%A4%A9%E7%BA%BF2.png) |

### [¶](#step-2-remove-the-power-supply) Step 2 - Remove the Power Supply

Use a Phillips screwdriver to loosen the 2 terminal screws and remove the Mainboard power cable from the Power Supply; Use a cross-head screwdriver to loosen the 3 terminal screws and remove the printer power cable from the Power Supply.

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![](/n1/a1-mini-base-housing/%E6%8B%A7%E6%9D%BE%E7%AB%AF%E5%8F%A3%E8%9E%BA%E4%B8%9D2%E9%A2%97.png) | ![](/n1/a1-mini-base-housing/%E6%8B%A7%E6%9D%BE%E6%89%93%E5%8D%B0%E6%9C%BA%E7%94%B5%E6%BA%90%E7%BA%BF%E8%9E%BA%E4%B8%9D.png) |

Unscrew 2 screws A and 1 screw B with an H2.0 Allen key and remove the Power Supply.

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![](/n1/a1-mini-base-housing/2_screws.jpg) | ![](/n1/a1-mini-base-housing/1_screw.jpg) |

### [¶](#step-3-remove-the-mainboard) Step 3 - Remove the Mainboard

Disconnect the Main Board Fan cable from the Mainboard and unscrew the 2 screws C with an H2.0 Allen key to remove the fan.

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![](/n1/a1-mini-base-housing/%E6%96%AD%E5%BC%80%E9%A3%8E%E6%89%87%E6%8E%92%E7%BA%BF.png) | ![](/n1/a1-mini-base-housing/%E5%8F%96%E5%87%BA%E9%A3%8E%E6%89%87%E8%9E%BA%E4%B8%9D2%E9%A2%97.png) |

Disconnect the Y motor cable, Z motor cable, and Y coil from the Mainboard and unscrew the 3 screws A with an H2.0 Allen key to remove the Mainboard.

|  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- |
| ![](/n1/a1-mini-base-housing/%E6%96%AD%E5%BC%80y%E7%94%B5%E6%9C%BA%E7%BA%BF%E3%80%81z%E7%94%B5%E6%9C%BA%E7%BA%BF%E5%92%8Cy%E7%BA%BF%E5%9C%88.png) | ![](/n1/a1-mini-base-housing/%E6%96%AD%E5%BC%80y%E7%94%B5%E6%9C%BA%E7%BA%BF%E3%80%81z%E7%94%B5%E6%9C%BA%E7%BA%BF%E5%92%8Cy%E7%BA%BF%E5%9C%88-3%E9%A2%97%E8%9E%BA%E4%B8%9D.png) | ![](/n1/a1-mini-base-housing/%E5%8F%96%E5%87%BA%E4%B8%BB%E6%9D%BF.png) |

### [¶](#step-4-remove-the-base-housing) Step 4 - Remove the Base Housing

Loosen the Y-axis (Eddy current) coil by removing 1 screw D with an H2.0 Allen key.

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![](/n1/a1-mini-base-housing/%E5%AE%89%E8%A3%85%E6%B6%A1%E6%B5%81%E6%97%8B%E5%9C%88%E5%B9%B6%E9%94%81%E5%85%A51%E9%A2%97%E8%9E%BA%E4%B8%9Df.png) | ![](/n1/a1-mini-base-housing/%E6%9D%BE%E5%BC%80y%E8%BD%B4%E6%B6%A1%E8%BD%AE%E6%97%8B%E5%9C%88.png) |

Remove the 2 screws E with an H2.0 Allen Key; Remove the Spool Holder quick-release plate; Remove the Z-axis trim cover with flat-tip tweezers.

|  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- |
| ![](/n1/a1-mini-base-housing/%E7%A7%BB%E9%99%A4%E6%96%99%E7%9B%98%E6%9E%B6%E5%BF%AB%E8%A3%85%E6%9D%BF%E8%9E%BA%E4%B8%9D.png) | ![](/n1/a1-mini-base-housing/%E7%A7%BB%E9%99%A4z%E8%BD%B4%E6%8E%A9%E9%A5%B0%E7%9B%96.png) | ![](/n1/a1-mini-base-housing/%E7%A7%BB%E9%99%A4z%E8%BD%B4%E6%8E%A9%E9%A5%B0%E7%9B%962.png) |

Remove 4 screws A and 1 screw F with an H2.0 Allen key, and then remove the Base Housing facing upwards along the Z column.

|  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- |
| ![](/n1/a1-mini-base-housing/%E6%9D%BE%E5%BC%80y%E5%BC%A0%E7%B4%A7%E5%BC%B9%E7%B0%A7%E8%9E%BA%E4%B8%9D.png) | ![](/n1/a1-mini-base-housing/%E6%9D%BE%E5%BC%80y%E5%BC%A0%E7%B4%A7%E5%BC%B9%E7%B0%A7%E8%9E%BA%E4%B8%9D2.png) | ![](/n1/a1-mini-base-housing/%E6%9D%BE%E5%BC%80y%E5%BC%A0%E7%B4%A7%E5%BC%B9%E7%B0%A7%E8%9E%BA%E4%B8%9D3.png) |

|  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- |
| ![](/n1/a1-mini-base-housing/%E6%9D%BE%E5%BC%80y%E5%BC%A0%E7%B4%A7%E5%BC%B9%E7%B0%A7%E8%9E%BA%E4%B8%9D4.png) | ![](/n1/a1-mini-base-housing/%E8%9E%BA%E4%B8%9Df.png) | ![](/n1/a1-mini-base-housing/%E5%8F%96%E5%87%BA%E5%BA%95%E5%BA%A7.png) |

### [¶](#step-5-remove-the-power-cable) Step 5 - Remove the Power Cable

Unplug the power cord from the switch with pliers and then remove the switch by pressing its side snap while pushing firmly outwards.

|  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- |
| ![](/n1/a1-mini-base-housing/%E7%A7%BB%E9%99%A4%E5%BC%80%E5%85%B3%E7%94%B5%E6%BA%90%E7%BA%BF.png) | ![](/n1/a1-mini-base-housing/%E7%A7%BB%E9%99%A4%E5%BC%80%E5%85%B3%E7%94%B5%E6%BA%90%E7%BA%BF2.png) | ![](/n1/a1-mini-base-housing/%E7%A7%BB%E9%99%A4%E5%BC%80%E5%85%B3%E7%94%B5%E6%BA%90%E7%BA%BF3.png) |

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![](/n1/a1-mini-base-housing/%E7%A7%BB%E9%99%A4%E7%94%B5%E6%BA%90%E5%BC%80%E5%85%B3.png) | ![](/n1/a1-mini-base-housing/%E7%A7%BB%E9%99%A4%E7%94%B5%E6%BA%90%E5%BC%80%E5%85%B32.png) |

## [¶](#assemble-the-base-housing) Assemble the Base Housing

### [¶](#step-1-install-the-power-cable) Step 1 - Install the Power Cable

Check the direction and install the power switch on the Base Housing.

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![](/n1/a1-mini-base-housing/%E5%AE%89%E8%A3%85%E5%BC%80%E5%85%B3.png) | ![](/n1/a1-mini-base-housing/%E5%AE%89%E8%A3%85%E5%BC%80%E5%85%B32.png) |

Connect the Power Cable to the switch with the short end of the cord underneath.

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![](/n1/a1-mini-base-housing/%E5%AE%89%E8%A3%85%E7%94%B5%E6%BA%90%E7%BA%BF.png) | ![](/n1/a1-mini-base-housing/%E5%AE%89%E8%A3%85%E7%94%B5%E6%BA%90%E7%BA%BF2.png) |

### [¶](#step-2-install-the-base-housing) Step 2 - Install the Base Housing

Mount the Base Housing to the Z-axis & Y-axis assembly in the Z-axis direction downwards, taking care to avoid the Y-axis coil.

|  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- |
| ![](/n1/a1-mini-base-housing/%E5%AE%89%E8%A3%85%E5%BA%95%E5%BA%A7.png) | ![](/n1/a1-mini-base-housing/%E5%AE%89%E8%A3%85%E5%BA%95%E5%BA%A73.png) | ![](/n1/a1-mini-base-housing/%E9%81%BF%E8%AE%A9y%E8%BD%B4%E6%B6%A1%E6%B5%81%E6%97%8B%E5%9C%88.png) |

Lock 1 screw F and 4 screws A to fix the Base Housing to the square steel.

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![](/n1/a1-mini-base-housing/%E9%94%81%E5%85%A5%E5%BA%95%E5%BA%A7%E8%9E%BA%E4%B8%9D.png) | ![](/n1/a1-mini-base-housing/%E9%94%81%E5%85%A5%E5%BA%95%E5%BA%A7%E8%9E%BA%E4%B8%9D2.png) |

Install the Y-axis (Eddy current) coil and lock in 1 screw D.

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![](/n1/a1-mini-base-housing/%E5%AE%89%E8%A3%85%E6%B6%A1%E6%B5%81%E6%97%8B%E5%9C%88.png) | ![](/n1/a1-mini-base-housing/%E5%AE%89%E8%A3%85%E6%B6%A1%E6%B5%81%E6%97%8B%E5%9C%88%E5%B9%B6%E9%94%81%E5%85%A51%E9%A2%97%E8%9E%BA%E4%B8%9Df.png) |

Mount the Z-axis trim cover, install the Spool Holder quick-release plate, and lock in the 2 screws E.

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![](/n1/a1-mini-base-housing/%E5%AE%89%E8%A3%85%E6%B6%A1%E6%B5%81%E6%97%8B%E5%9C%88.png) | ![](/n1/a1-mini-base-housing/%E5%AE%89%E8%A3%85%E6%B6%A1%E6%B5%81%E6%97%8B%E5%9C%88%E5%B9%B6%E9%94%81%E5%85%A51%E9%A2%97%E8%9E%BA%E4%B8%9Df.png) |

### [¶](#step-3-install-the-mainboard) Step 3 - Install the Mainboard

Install the Mainboard on the Base Housing and lock in 3 screws A to secure it.

|  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- |
| ![](/n1/a1-mini-base-housing/%E5%AE%89%E8%A3%85%E4%B8%BB%E6%9D%BF.png) | ![](/n1/a1-mini-base-housing/%E5%AE%89%E8%A3%85%E4%B8%BB%E6%9D%BF3.png) | ![](/n1/a1-mini-base-housing/%E5%AE%89%E8%A3%85%E4%B8%BB%E6%9D%BF2.png) |

Connect the Y-coil, Z-motor and Y-motor cables to the corresponding connectors; Manage the cables.

|  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- |
| ![](/n1/a1-mini-base-housing/%E8%BF%9E%E6%8E%A5y%E7%BA%BF%E5%9C%88%E3%80%81z%E7%94%B5%E6%9C%BA%E5%92%8Cy%E7%94%B5%E6%9C%BA%E7%94%B5%E7%BC%861.png) | ![](/n1/a1-mini-base-housing/%E8%BF%9E%E6%8E%A5y%E7%BA%BF%E5%9C%88%E3%80%81z%E7%94%B5%E6%9C%BA%E5%92%8Cy%E7%94%B5%E6%9C%BA%E7%94%B5%E7%BC%862.png) | ![](/n1/a1-mini-base-housing/%E8%BF%9E%E6%8E%A5y%E7%BA%BF%E5%9C%88%E3%80%81z%E7%94%B5%E6%9C%BA%E5%92%8Cy%E7%94%B5%E6%9C%BA%E7%94%B5%E7%BC%863.png) |

Install the Main Board Fan, lock in 2 screws C to fix the fan, and connect the fan cable .

|  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- |
| ![](/n1/a1-mini-base-housing/%E5%AE%89%E8%A3%85%E4%B8%BB%E6%9D%BF%E9%A3%8E%E6%89%87.png) | ![](/n1/a1-mini-base-housing/%E5%AE%89%E8%A3%85%E4%B8%BB%E6%9D%BF%E9%A3%8E%E6%89%872.png) | ![](/n1/a1-mini-base-housing/%E5%AE%89%E8%A3%85%E4%B8%BB%E6%9D%BF%E9%A3%8E%E6%89%873.png) |

### [¶](#step-4-install-the-power-supply) Step 4 - Install the Power Supply

Install the Power Supply module and secure it with 2 screws A and 1 screw B.

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![](/n1/a1-mini-base-housing/2_screws.jpg) | ![](/n1/a1-mini-base-housing/1_screw.jpg) |

Connect the Mainboard power cables to the output terminals of the Power Supply Module, with the red wire connected to the positive terminal and the black wire to the negative one, and then tighten the 2 screws to fix it.

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![](/n1/a1-mini-base-housing/%E5%B0%86%E4%B8%BB%E6%9D%BF%E7%94%B5%E6%BA%90%E7%BA%BF%E8%BF%9E%E6%8E%A5%E5%88%B0%E7%94%B5%E6%BA%90%E6%A8%A1%E5%9D%97%E7%9A%84%E8%BE%93%E5%87%BA%E7%AB%AF%E5%AD%90%E4%B8%8A.png) | ![](/n1/a1-mini-base-housing/%E5%B0%86%E4%B8%BB%E6%9D%BF%E7%94%B5%E6%BA%90%E7%BA%BF%E8%BF%9E%E6%8E%A5%E5%88%B0%E7%94%B5%E6%BA%90%E6%A8%A1%E5%9D%97%E7%9A%84%E8%BE%93%E5%87%BA%E7%AB%AF%E5%AD%90%E4%B8%8A2.png) |

Connect the Power Cable to the input terminals, with the brown wire connected to L, blue wire connected to N, and yellow wire connected to the ground, and then tighten the 3 screws.

|  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- |
| ![](/n1/a1-mini-base-housing/%E5%B0%86%E7%94%B5%E6%BA%90%E7%BA%BF%E8%BF%9E%E6%8E%A5%E5%88%B0%E8%BE%93%E5%85%A5%E7%AB%AF%E5%AD%90%E4%B8%8A%EF%BC%8C%E6%A3%95%E8%89%B2%E7%BA%BF%E6%8E%A5l_%E8%93%9D%E8%89%B2%E7%BA%BF%E6%8E%A5n_%E9%BB%84%E8%89%B2%E7%BA%BF%E6%8E%A5%E5%9C%B0.png) | ![](/n1/a1-mini-base-housing/%E5%B0%86%E7%94%B5%E6%BA%90%E7%BA%BF%E8%BF%9E%E6%8E%A5%E5%88%B0%E8%BE%93%E5%85%A5%E7%AB%AF%E5%AD%90%E4%B8%8A%EF%BC%8C%E6%A3%95%E8%89%B2%E7%BA%BF%E6%8E%A5l_%E8%93%9D%E8%89%B2%E7%BA%BF%E6%8E%A5n_%E9%BB%84%E8%89%B2%E7%BA%BF%E6%8E%A5%E5%9C%B02.png) | ![](/n1/a1-mini-base-housing/%E5%B0%86%E7%94%B5%E6%BA%90%E7%BA%BF%E8%BF%9E%E6%8E%A5%E5%88%B0%E8%BE%93%E5%85%A5%E7%AB%AF%E5%AD%90%E4%B8%8A%EF%BC%8C%E6%A3%95%E8%89%B2%E7%BA%BF%E6%8E%A5l_%E8%93%9D%E8%89%B2%E7%BA%BF%E6%8E%A5n_%E9%BB%84%E8%89%B2%E7%BA%BF%E6%8E%A5%E5%9C%B03.png) |

### [¶](#step-5-install-the-touch-screen-and-wifi-antenna) Step 5 - Install the Touch Screen and Wifi Antenna

Attach the Wifi antenna with the side with adhesives to the Base Housing; Connect the connecting cable to the Mainboard.

|  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- |
| ![](/n1/a1-mini-base-housing/%E5%AE%89%E8%A3%85wifi%E5%A4%A9%E7%BA%BF.png) | ![](/n1/a1-mini-base-housing/%E5%AE%89%E8%A3%85wifi%E5%A4%A9%E7%BA%BF2.png) | ![](/n1/a1-mini-base-housing/%E5%AE%89%E8%A3%85wifi%E5%A4%A9%E7%BA%BF3.png) |

Remove the protective paper, mount the display and connect the wires to the Mainboard.

|  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- |
| ![](/n1/a1-mini-base-housing/%E5%8E%BB%E9%99%A4%E5%B1%8F%E5%B9%95%E4%BF%9D%E6%8A%A4%E7%BA%B8.png) | ![](/n1/a1-mini-base-housing/%E5%AE%89%E8%A3%85%E5%B1%8F%E5%B9%95.png) | ![](/n1/a1-mini-base-housing/%E5%AE%89%E8%A3%85%E5%B1%8F%E5%B9%952.png) |

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![](/n1/a1-mini-base-housing/%E5%AE%89%E8%A3%85%E5%B1%8F%E5%B9%95fpc.png) | ![](/n1/a1-mini-base-housing/%E5%AE%89%E8%A3%85%E5%B1%8F%E5%B9%95fpc2.png) |

## [¶](#verify-the-functionality) Verify the Functionality

Check and ensure that the connecting wires are not plugged in incorrectly or missed.

## [¶](#calibration) Calibration

Not required

A1 mini installation tutorial

This article provides guidelines and precautions for disassembling and assembling the A1 Mini

---

## [¶](#a1-mini-excluding-the-toolhead) A1 mini （Excluding the Toolhead）

This article provides guidelines and precautions for disassembling and assembling the A1 mini, and the disassembly and assembly of replaceable components involved in this process can also be referenced in this article.

![](/n1/com.jpg)

The following are the after-sales spare parts that will be covered in this guideline.

| 4 | Bambu USB-C Cable | ![](/n1/a1-mini-printer/usb-c%E6%95%B0%E6%8D%AE%E7%BA%BF.png) | 5 | X Motor Cover | ![](/n1/a1-mini-printer/x%E7%94%B5%E6%9C%BA%E7%BD%A9.png) | 6 | Live View Camera | ![](/n1/a1-mini-printer/%E6%91%84%E5%83%8F%E5%A4%B4.png) |

## [¶](#tool-list) Tool  List

* H2.0/H1.5 Allen Key
* Wire Cutters
* Needle-nose Pliers

## [¶](#safety-warning) Safety Warning

> **Important Reminder！**
>
> Before performing any maintenance work on the printer and its electronic devices (including Tool head cable), please turn off the printer's power and disconnect the power supply to avoid circuit short-circuits that may cause additional damage to electronic devices and potential safety hazards.
>
> When conducting maintenance or troubleshooting on the printer, please first ensure that the hotend and heated bed are at a safe temperature. Avoid operating them at high temperatures. If it is necessary to perform tasks at high temperatures, please wear appropriate heat-resistant gloves to ensure safe and effective maintenance work
>
> If you have any questions about this guide, please [Click here to submit a service ticket](https://bambulab.cn/zh-cn/sign-in?to=%2Fmy%2Fsupport%2Ftickets), and we will respond promptly and provide the assistance you need.

## [¶](#video-guide) Video Guide

Due to differences in product versions, there may be some differences in details.

## [¶](#disassembly-guide) Disassembly Guide

### [¶](#step-1-remove-the-baseplate) Step 1: Remove the Baseplate

Tilt the printer backward and remove 2 screws A. Remove the cable buckle.

Remove 14 screws B, then pull and stretch the nylon ties to loosen the baseplate and then remove it.

|  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- |
| ![](/n1/a1-mini-printer/b-2-screws.jpg) | ![](/n1/a1-mini-printer/b-14-screws.jpg) | ![](/n1/a1-mini-printer/pull_cables.jpg) |

### [¶](#step-2-disconnect-the-cables) Step 2: Disconnect the cables

Use wire cutters to cut the zip ties, remove 2 screws C and plate, and disconnect the USB cable, camera cable, and X motor cable from the mainboard.

|  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- |
| ![](/n1/a1-mini-printer/%E5%89%AA%E6%96%AD%E6%89%8E%E5%B8%A6.png) | ![](/n1/a1-mini-printer/%E6%96%AD%E5%BC%80%E6%89%8E%E5%B8%A6-9.png) | ![](/n1/a1-mini-printer/disconnect_3_cables.jpg) |

### [¶](#step-3remove-the-heatbed) Step 3：Remove the Heatbed

Use an H2.0 hex wrench to remove 3 screws B, remove the fireproof cover, use pliers to remove the two terminal insulating rubber sleeves,

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![](/n1/a1-mini-printer/b-3-cover.jpg) | ![](/n1/a1-mini-printer/rubbers.jpg) |

Use a wrench to unlock the plug and unplug the 2 heatbed power cords at the same time; Disconnect the connection of the heatbed signal cable from the mainboard, remove 1 screw A, and loosen the heatbed cable from the cable buckle.

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![](/n1/a1-mini-printer/disconnect_cables.jpg) | ![](/n1/a1-mini-printer/disconnect_cable_1.jpg) |

Place the printer upright and remove 3 screws D that secure the heat bed.

|  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- |
| ![](/n1/a1-mini-printer/%E7%A7%BB%E9%99%A4%E7%83%AD%E5%BA%8A10.png) | ![](/n1/a1-mini-printer/%E7%A7%BB%E9%99%A4%E7%83%AD%E5%BA%8A14.png) | ![](/n1/a1-mini-printer/%E7%A7%BB%E9%99%A4%E7%83%AD%E5%BA%8A15.png) |

Press down on the heat bed with your hand and remove 4 leveling screws E. Take off the heat bed along with the adjustment springs.

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![](/n1/a1-mini-printer/%E7%A7%BB%E9%99%A4%E7%83%AD%E5%BA%8A11.png) | ![](/n1/a1-mini-printer/%E7%A7%BB%E9%99%A4%E7%83%AD%E5%BA%8A13.png) |

### [¶](#step-4-remove-the-usb-cable-camera-and-x-motor-cover) Step 4: Remove the USB cable, camera, and X motor cover

Remove the PTFE tube and USB cable from the Cable Organizer. Press the connector to disconnect the tube, and then remove the PTFE Tube Connector.

|  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- |
| ![](/n1/a1-mini-printer/usb_and_tube.jpg) | ![](/n1/a1-mini-printer/usb-2.png) | ![](/n1/a1-mini-printer/usb-3.png) |

Apply force from below the motor cover front lid to remove it. Disconnect the camera cable. Insert an Allen wrench through the cable clamp and pry it out. Remove the cable clamp.

|  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- |
| ![](/n1/a1-mini-printer/motor_cover.jpg) | ![](/n1/a1-mini-printer/usb-5.png) | ![](/n1/a1-mini-printer/release_the_buckle.jpg) |

Remove the cable buckle left on the cables, tear off the tape at one end of the nylon mesh, and pull out the camera connection cable and X motor cable;

|  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- |
| ![](/n1/a1-mini-printer/usb-7.png) | ![](/n1/a1-mini-printer/usb-8.png) | ![](/n1/a1-mini-printer/remove_cable.jpg) |

Remove the ferrite cores, pull out the rubber plug on the USB cable from the motor cover, and remove the USB cable;

There are two versions of the USB-C ferrite cores: the split/separated structure shown in the image and an integrated one-piece ferrite core.

If it is the integrated one-piece structure, you do not need to install the separated/split type of ferrite core onto the cable, as the ferrite core is already integrated into the cable.

The two versions are shipped randomly. The final product received should be considered the authoritative version.

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![](/n1/a1-mini-printer/usb-11.png) | ![](/n1/a1-mini-printer/usb-12.png) |

Remove 1 screw F, 2 screws G, and take off the X motor cover assembly;

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![](/n1/a1-mini-printer/usb-13.png) | ![](/n1/a1-mini-printer/usb-14.png) |

Press the direction of the arrow to unlock the camera and remove the camera from the X motor cover.

|  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- |
| ![](/n1/a1-mini-printer/camera.jpg) | ![](/n1/a1-mini-printer/usb-16.png) | ![](/n1/a1-mini-printer/usb-17.png) |

### [¶](#step-5-remove-the-x-motor) Step 5: Remove the X Motor

Remove 2 screws F and remove the motor rear cover. Remove 4 screws I and take down the X motor

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![](/n1/a1-mini-printer/%E7%A7%BB%E9%99%A4x%E7%94%B5%E6%9C%BA-1.png) | ![](/n1/a1-mini-printer/%E7%A7%BB%E9%99%A4x%E7%94%B5%E6%9C%BA-2.png) |

## [¶](#step-6-remove-the-x-axis-assembly) Step 6: Remove the X-axis assembly.

Remove 4 screws J, take down the X-axis module; remove 4  screws, remove 2 screws K, and take down the adapter bracket.

|  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- |
| ![](/n1/a1-mini-printer/%E7%A7%BB%E9%99%A4x%E7%BB%84%E4%BB%B6.png) | ![](/n1/a1-mini-printer/%E7%A7%BB%E9%99%A4x%E7%BB%84%E4%BB%B6-2.png) | ![](/n1/a1-mini-printer/%E7%A7%BB%E9%99%A4x%E7%BB%84%E4%BB%B6-3.png) |

### [¶](#step-7-remove-the-y-axis-linear-rail) Step 7: Remove the Y-axis Linear Rail

Push the heatbed bracket backward to expose the 2 screws on the connecting plate and loosen the screws completely, then remove the 2 screws L on the pulley and take down the connecting plate;

|  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- |
| ![](/n1/a1-mini-printer/%E7%A7%BB%E9%99%A4y%E8%BD%B4%E7%BA%BF%E8%BD%A8-2.png) | ![](/n1/a1-mini-printer/%E7%A7%BB%E9%99%A4y%E8%BD%B4%E7%BA%BF4.png) | ![](/n1/a1-mini-printer/%E7%A7%BB%E9%99%A4y%E8%BD%B4%E7%BA%BF6.png) |

Remove 2 screws M and take off the Y-axis rear cover.

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![](/n1/a1-mini-printer/%E7%A7%BB%E9%99%A4y%E8%BD%B4%E7%BA%BF7.png) | ![](/n1/a1-mini-printer/%E7%A7%BB%E9%99%A4y%E8%BD%B4%E7%BA%BF%E8%BD%A8-8.png) |

Remove 4 screws N and remove the Y-axis linear rail.

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![](/n1/a1-mini-printer/%E7%A7%BB%E9%99%A4y%E8%BD%B4%E7%BA%BF%E8%BD%A8-9.png) | ![](/n1/a1-mini-printer/%E7%A7%BB%E9%99%A4y%E8%BD%B4%E7%BA%BF%E8%BD%A8-10.png) |

## [¶](#assembly-guide) Assembly Guide

### [¶](#step-1-install-the-y-axis-linear-rail) Step 1: Install the Y-axis Linear Rail

Use tweezers to move the Y belt pressing block to expose the 2 screws. Confirm the installation direction according to the installation position of the connecting plate, place the Y-axis linear rail on the base, align the screw holes, put in 4 screws N, and lock them alternately;

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![](/n1/a1-mini-printer/move_belt_holder.jpg) | ![](/n1/a1-mini-printer/%E5%AE%89%E8%A3%85y%E8%BD%B4%E7%BA%BF%E8%BD%A8-4.png) |

Move the heatbed bracket to expose the Y-axis belt block, then install the connecting plate, confirm that it is in place, and lock 2 screws L to fix the connecting plate;

|  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- |
| ![](/n1/a1-mini-printer/y_belt_holder_2.jpg) | ![](/n1/a1-mini-printer/plate.jpg) | ![](/n1/a1-mini-printer/%E7%A7%BB%E9%99%A4y%E8%BD%B4%E7%BA%BF4.png) |

Then tighten the 2 screws through the base, move the heatbed bracket, and confirm that the heatbed bracket can drive the Y belt to move smoothly.

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![](/n1/a1-mini-printer/%E5%AE%89%E8%A3%85y%E8%BD%B4%E7%BA%BF%E8%BD%A8-8.png) | ![](/n1/a1-mini-printer/move_y.jpg) |

Install the Y-axis rear cover, and lock in 2 screws M.

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![](https://wiki.bambulab.com/n1/a1-mini-printer/%E7%A7%BB%E9%99%A4y%E8%BD%B4%E7%BA%BF%E8%BD%A8-8.png) | ![](https://wiki.bambulab.com/n1/a1-mini-printer/%E7%A7%BB%E9%99%A4y%E8%BD%B4%E7%BA%BF7.png) |

### [¶](#step-2-install-the-x-axis-assembly) Step 2: Install the X-axis assembly.

Install the adapter bracket, lock 4 silver screws on the front and 2 screws K on the back;

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![](/n1/a1-mini-printer/%E5%AE%89%E8%A3%85x%E7%BB%84%E4%BB%B6-1.png) | ![](/n1/a1-mini-printer/%E5%AE%89%E8%A3%85x%E7%BB%84%E4%BB%B6-2.png) |

Install the X-axis module, taking care to prevent the X slider from falling off, and then lock the 4 screws J. Be careful not to press the X-axis timing belt when tightening the lower 2 screws.

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![](/n1/a1-mini-printer/%E5%AE%89%E8%A3%85x%E7%BB%84%E4%BB%B6-3.png) | ![](/n1/a1-mini-printer/%E5%AE%89%E8%A3%85x%E7%BB%84%E4%BB%B6-6.png) |

### [¶](#step-3-install-the-x-motor) Step 3: Install the X motor

Install the X motor with the motor cable facing downwards and lock in the 4 screws G.

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![](/n1/a1-mini-printer/%E5%AE%89%E8%A3%85x%E7%94%B5%E6%9C%BA.png) | ![](/n1/a1-mini-printer/%E5%AE%89%E8%A3%85x%E7%94%B5%E6%9C%BA-1.png) |

### [¶](#step-4-install-the-x-motor-cover-camera-and-usb-cable) Step 4: Install the X motor cover, camera, and USB cable

Install the camera onto the motor cover. Thread the USB cable and fasten it to the motor cover.

|  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- |
| ![](/n1/a1-mini-printer/%E5%AE%89%E8%A3%85usb.png) | ![](/n1/a1-mini-printer/%E5%AE%89%E8%A3%85usb-1.png) | ![](/n1/a1-mini-printer/%E5%AE%89%E8%A3%85usb-2.png) |

Pass the X-axis synchronous belt around the motor driving wheel, and then install the motor cover to the adapter bracket in the direction of the arrow. Note that the motor cable must pass through the motor cover. Lock 1 screw F on the X-axis and lock it inside the motor cover. 2 screws G secure the motor cover;

|  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- |
| ![](/n1/a1-mini-printer/%E5%AE%89%E8%A3%85usb-3.png) | ![](/n1/a1-mini-printer/%E5%AE%89%E8%A3%85usb-4.png) | ![](/n1/a1-mini-printer/%E5%AE%89%E8%A3%85usb-5.png) |

Connect the small end of the camera cable to the camera, and then pass the USB cable, X motor cable and camera cable through the nylon mesh in sequence;

|  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- |
| ![](/n1/a1-mini-printer/%E5%AE%89%E8%A3%85usb-6.png) | ![](/n1/a1-mini-printer/%E5%AE%89%E8%A3%85usb-7.png) | ![](/n1/a1-mini-printer/%E5%AE%89%E8%A3%85usb-10.png) |

Confirm the direction of the wire clamp, install the wire clamp on the motor cover, then confirm the installation direction, and cover the front cover of the motor cover;

|  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- |
| ![](/n1/a1-mini-printer/clip.jpg) | ![](/n1/a1-mini-printer/clips.jpg) | ![](/n1/a1-mini-printer/x_front_cover.jpg) |

Install the rear cover of the X motor cover , lock in 2 screws H, align the installation position, and install the PTFE Tube Connector;

|  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- |
| ![](/n1/a1-mini-printer/%E5%AE%89%E8%A3%85usb-13.png) | ![](/n1/a1-mini-printer/rear_cover.jpg) | ![](/n1/a1-mini-printer/connector.jpg) |

Wrap the other end of the nylon mesh with tape and install a ferrite cores about 25 mm away from the USB connector.

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![](/n1/a1-mini-printer/%E5%AE%89%E8%A3%85usb-15.png) | ![](/n1/a1-mini-printer/length.jpg) |

### [¶](#step-5-install-the-heatbed) Step 5: Install the Heatbed.

Pre-fix the four springs to the corresponding positions with glue, align the spring positions, place the heatbed, and confirm from the bottom of the heatbed that the three fixing pieces on the heatbed are all on the outside of the heatbed bracket;

|  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- |
| ![](/n1/a1-mini-printer/%E5%AE%89%E8%A3%85%E7%83%AD%E5%BA%8A.png) | ![](/n1/a1-mini-printer/%E5%AE%89%E8%A3%85%E7%83%AD%E5%BA%8A-2.png) | ![](/n1/a1-mini-printer/position.jpg) |

Insert 4 leveling screws E and compress the leveling spring of the heatbed by hand so that the leveling screws can be locked. After all are locked, loosen the 4 leveling screws by 1 turn; install 3 heatbed secure screws D, but do not tighten them. These 3 screws need to be tightened after completing the manual leveling of the heatbed;

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![](/n1/a1-mini-printer/%E5%AE%89%E8%A3%85%E7%83%AD%E5%BA%8A-3.png) | ![](/n1/a1-mini-printer/%E5%AE%89%E8%A3%85%E7%83%AD%E5%BA%8A-6.png) |

Tilt the printer backwards, then connect the heatbed power cord as shown in the picture, pay attention to distinguish the red and black cables, install the insulating rubber sleeves, and then connect the heatbed signal cable;

|  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- |
| ![](/n1/a1-mini-printer/heatbed_cable_cn.jpg) | ![](/n1/a1-mini-printer/rubber.jpg) | ![](/n1/a1-mini-printer/%E5%AE%89%E8%A3%85%E7%83%AD%E5%BA%8A-12.png) |

Install the cable buckle and use 2 screws A to lock the heatbed wire and power cable;

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![](/n1/a1-mini-printer/%E5%AE%89%E8%A3%85%E7%83%AD%E5%BA%8A-13.png) | ![](/n1/a1-mini-printer/%E5%AE%89%E8%A3%85%E7%83%AD%E5%BA%8A-14.png) |

Arrange the heatbed cables and power cable, install the fireproof cover, confirm that the cables are not pressed, and then lock in the 3 screws B.

![](/n1/a1-mini-printer/%E5%AE%89%E8%A3%85%E7%83%AD%E5%BA%8A-15.png)

### [¶](#step-6-connect-the-cables) Step 6: Connect the cables

Connect the X motor cable and camera cable to the mainboard, connect the USB cable, after confirming the location of the ferrite cores, lock the pressing piece with 2 screws C. Arrange the cables and tie them with small zip ties.

There are two versions of the USB-C ferrite cores: the split/separated structure shown in the image and an integrated one-piece ferrite core.

If it is the integrated one-piece structure, you do not need to install the separated/split type of ferrite core onto the cable, as the ferrite core is already integrated into the cable.

The two versions are shipped randomly. The final product received should be considered the authoritative version.

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![](/n1/a1-mini-printer/disconnect_3_cables.jpg) | ![](/n1/a1-mini-printer/location_of_the_ring.jpg) |

|  |  |
| --- | --- |
| ![](/n1/a1-mini-printer/%E8%BF%9E%E6%8E%A5%E7%94%B5%E7%BC%86-4.png) | ![](/n1/a1-mini-printer/ties.jpg) |

### [¶](#step-7-install-the-baseplate) Step 7: Install the Baseplate

Install the baseplate, align the position, and lock in 14 screws B to fix it; then install the cable clamp, lock in 2 screws A to fix it; place the printer upright.

|  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- |
| ![](/n1/a1-mini-printer/%E5%AE%89%E8%A3%85%E5%BA%95%E6%9D%BF.png) | ![](/n1/a1-mini-printer/%E5%AE%89%E8%A3%85%E5%BA%95%E6%9D%BF-2.png) | ![](/n1/a1-mini-printer/%E6%AD%A3%E6%94%BE%E6%89%93%E5%8D%B0%E6%9C%BA.png) |

## [¶](#verify-the-functionality) Verify the functionality

After installing the tool head, connect the USB cable, start the printer, and confirm that the printer can start normally and the screen can display normally.

## [¶](#calibration) Calibration

After disassembling and assembling the heatbed, you need to manually level the heatbed. Please click on [Manual Leveling of the Heatbed](/en/a1-mini/maintenance/manual-bed-tramming) for specific operations;
